 workers' useful autonomous web, full authenticated admin and all seven product
 journeys still require their mandatory actual checks. Direct ordinary

+
+
+## Ordinary native skills integration candidate
+
+The new `user-skills.patch` keeps Hermes' native skill files, manager actions,
+frontmatter validation, static scan/lint, naming, history and batch semantics.
+Newly approved ordinary templates include `skills_list`, `skill_view` and
+`skill_manage`; existing grants are not enlarged or migrated automatically.
+Personal roots and supporting documents use bounded descriptor I/O and current
+principal/generation checks. Prompt, slash/autoload and list/view projections
+share those readers. Scoped reads rebuild from current private bytes instead
+of accepting the donor's stat-only or persistent snapshots as authority.
+
+Reading instructions is passive: it does not install declared packages, run
+inline shell, capture secrets, register global environment passthrough or
+import/discover plugins. Unverified execution/dependency/mount requirements
+are returned as setup needed. Native unscoped donor behavior remains present.
+Commands and scripts can be read; execution still requires the existing
+admitted task path and the original task budget.
+
+Remaining ordinary-user gaps are explicit: admission for project/external and
+plugin skill libraries, inline-shell/package activation, scoped credential
+mounts, and the ordinary slash admission route. These are not accepted feature
+exclusions. Mixed privileged unscoped writes into a managed ordinary skill
+home need the same publication-lock proof before release. Source/offline
+integration, installed execution and release acceptance remain separate.

++This policy supplies no network/containment admission. Native agent-browser 0.26

++class BrowserPolicyError(PermissionError):
++    pass
++
++
++# A command clock is a ceiling, never execution authority. The existing owner
++# must eventually supply the stricter original job/cgroup clock as well.
++_BROWSER_DEADLINE = contextvars.ContextVar('friday_browser_deadline', default=None)
++
++
++def command_deadline(timeout):
++    if isinstance(timeout, bool) or not isinstance(timeout, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(timeout) or timeout <= 0:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_finite_command_budget_required')
++    proposed = time.monotonic() + timeout
++    retained = _BROWSER_DEADLINE.get()
++    return min(proposed, retained) if retained is not None else proposed
++
++
++def check_command_deadline(deadline=None):
++    retained = _BROWSER_DEADLINE.get()
++    if retained is not None:
++        deadline = min(retained, deadline) if deadline is not None else retained
++    if (isinstance(deadline, bool) or not isinstance(deadline, (int, float))
++            or not math.isfinite(deadline)):
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_original_deadline_required')
++    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_original_deadline_exhausted')
++    return deadline
++
++
++def require_execution_admission():
++    """Reconcile the actual native owner, then require the still-missing network route.
++
++    This is connected to the retained WebCall/UserScope and existing registry.
++    There is deliberately no bool/config/token producer for page/CDP egress.
++    """
++    from tools.browser_tool_owner import current_browser_owner
++    from tools.process_registry import process_registry
++    try:
++        owner = current_browser_owner()
++    except (RuntimeError, PermissionError):
++        raise BrowserPolicyError("browser_execution_boundary_unavailable: REQUIRED_UNRESOLVED_ACTUAL_NATIVE_OWNER") from None
++    try:
++        process_registry.require_browser_execution(owner)
++    except RuntimeError as exc:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError(str(exc)) from None
++    return owner
++
++
++def check_managed_execution(deadline=None):
++    from tools.interrupt import is_interrupted
++    if is_interrupted():
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_interrupted')
++    if local_policy() is None:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_local_policy_required')
++    require_sandbox_host()
++    from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import current
++    current()  # Retained ordinary admission is necessary but not a network grant.
++    check_command_deadline(deadline)
++    return require_execution_admission()
++
++
++@contextlib.contextmanager
++def managed_browser_operation(timeout, *, task_id=None):
++    deadline = command_deadline(timeout)
++    token = _BROWSER_DEADLINE.set(deadline)
++    try:
++        owner = check_managed_execution(deadline)
++        if owner is not None:
++            owner.require_route_task(task_id)
++            deadline = min(deadline, owner.check())
++            _BROWSER_DEADLINE.set(deadline)
++            check_command_deadline(deadline)
++        yield deadline
++    finally:
++        _BROWSER_DEADLINE.reset(token)
++
++
++def browser_action_entry(fn=None, *, timeout_fn=None):
++    """Same execution fence for facade, separate tools and direct driver routes.
++
++    Availability/check_fn, operator identity and an existing supervisor are not
++    admission. Keep legacy APIs when no managed policy is selected; a managed
++    call retains its original ceiling across nested sync/async routing.
++    """
++    from functools import wraps
++    import inspect
++    if fn is None:
++        return lambda target: browser_action_entry(target, timeout_fn=timeout_fn)
++    signature = inspect.signature(fn)
++
++    def operation(args, kwargs):
++        if timeout_fn is not None:
++            timeout = timeout_fn()
++        else:
++            bound = signature.bind_partial(*args, **kwargs)
++            bound.apply_defaults()
++            timeout = bound.arguments.get('timeout', bound.arguments.get('timeout_s', 30.0))
++        bound = signature.bind_partial(*args, **kwargs)
++        return managed_browser_operation(timeout, task_id=bound.arguments.get("task_id"))
++
++    if inspect.iscoroutinefunction(fn):
++        @wraps(fn)
++        async def async_call(*args, **kwargs):
++            if local_policy() is None:
++                return await fn(*args, **kwargs)
++            with operation(args, kwargs):
++                return await fn(*args, **kwargs)
++        return async_call
++
++    @wraps(fn)
++    def call(*args, **kwargs):
++        if local_policy() is None:
++            return fn(*args, **kwargs)
++        with operation(args, kwargs):
++            return fn(*args, **kwargs)
++    return call
++
++
++def friday_browser_config():
++    return copy.deepcopy(FRIDAY_BROWSER)
++
++
++def local_policy(config=None):
++    """Opt-in policy, read per profile/command. Ordinary scopes cannot downgrade it."""
++    if config is None:
++        from hermes_cli.config import read_raw_config
++        config = read_raw_config()
++    browser = config.get('browser', {})
++    from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import _CURRENT
++    if not isinstance(browser, dict):
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_policy_invalid')
++    if 'local_runtime' not in browser:
++        if _CURRENT.get() is not None:
++            raise BrowserPolicyError('ordinary_browser_policy_required')
++        if _BROWSER_DEADLINE.get() is not None:
++            raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_retained_policy_required')
++        return None
++    if any(browser.get(key) != value for key, value in FRIDAY_BROWSER.items()):
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_local_policy_changed')
++    # Unknown launch overrides are not read by this policy, but reject known
++    # alternative drivers before provider/CDP/profile resolution has effects.
++    return browser['local_runtime']
++
++
++def require_sandbox_host():
++    """Match EVERY automatic no-sandbox condition of the exact 0.26 source."""
++    if os.name != 'posix' or not hasattr(os, 'geteuid') or os.geteuid() == 0:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_sandbox_requires_nonroot_posix')
++    if 'CI' in os.environ or any(Path(p).exists() for p in ('/.dockerenv', '/run/.containerenv')):
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_automatic_no_sandbox_host_refused')
++    try:
++        cgroup = Path('/proc/1/cgroup').read_text()
++    except OSError:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_container_status_unknown') from None
++    if any(token in cgroup for token in ('docker', 'kubepods', 'lxc')):
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_automatic_no_sandbox_host_refused')
++    if any(name.startswith(('AGENT_BROWSER_', 'BROWSER_', 'BROWSERBASE_', 'BROWSER_USE_', 'CAMOFOX_',
++                            'FIRECRAWL_')) for name in os.environ):
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_ambient_configuration_refused')
++
++
++def _pinned_executable(path, expected, *, root_owned=False):
++    path = Path(path)
++    if not path.is_absolute() or path.resolve() != path:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_executable_path_refused')
++    uid = 0 if root_owned else os.geteuid()
++    for parent in path.parents:
++        st = parent.lstat()
++        if not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid not in (0, uid) or st.st_mode & 0o022:
++            raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_executable_ancestor_refused')
++    fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK)
++    try:
++        st = os.fstat(fd)
++        if (not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid not in (0, uid)
++                or st.st_nlink != 1 or st.st_mode & 0o022 or not st.st_mode & 0o111
++                or not 0 < st.st_size <= 512 * 1024 * 1024):
++            raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_executable_protection_refused')
++        digest = hashlib.sha256()
++        with os.fdopen(fd, 'rb', closefd=False) as stream:
++            for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
++                digest.update(block)
++        after = path.stat()
++        fields = ('st_dev', 'st_ino', 'st_size', 'st_mtime_ns', 'st_ctime_ns', 'st_mode', 'st_uid')
++        if digest.hexdigest() != expected or any(getattr(after, k) != getattr(st, k) for k in fields):
++            raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_executable_pin_changed')
++    finally:
++        os.close(fd)
++    return str(path)
++
++
++def local_readiness(config=None):
++    """No exec, install, version subprocess or PATH fallback; errors are fatal."""
++    policy = local_policy(config)
++    if policy is None:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_local_policy_required')
++    require_sandbox_host()
++    executable = _pinned_executable(policy['executable'], policy['executable_sha256'], root_owned=True)
++    installed = pm.installed_package('agent-browser', read_only=True)
++    if installed is None or installed.version != policy['agent_browser_version'] or installed.binary is None:
++        raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_exact_pm_cli_required')
++    cli = _pinned_executable(installed.binary, policy['agent_browser_sha256'])
++    return {'executable': executable, 'cli': cli}
++
+ 
+ def chromium_executable(*, allow_override: bool = True) -> str | None:
+-    """Prefer an explicit override unless the caller needs PM's own Chromium."""
+-    override = os.environ.get("AGENT_BROWSER_EXECUTABLE_PATH") if allow_override else None
++    policy = local_policy()

++        # Even callers requesting PM-only must not silently change the selected
++        # protected build. Real-profile/CDP drivers are refused by this policy.
++        return _pinned_executable(policy['executable'], policy['executable_sha256'], root_owned=True)
++    override = os.environ.get('AGENT_BROWSER_EXECUTABLE_PATH') if allow_override else None
+     if override:
+         return override
+-    installed = pm.installed_package("chromium")
++    installed = pm.installed_package('chromium')
+     return str(installed.binary) if installed and installed.binary is not None else None

++def managed_command_environment(socket_dir, idle_seconds, *, deadline=None):
++    """Private native profile HOME; no inherited flags, proxy, config or secrets."""
++    check_managed_execution(deadline)
++    selected = local_readiness()
++    check_managed_execution(deadline)

++    from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import current, _private
++    cap = current()
++    home = get_hermes_home()
++    _private(home, directory=True)
++    generation = str(cap.admission_generation) if cap is not None else 'operator'
++    root = home / 'browser'
++    private_home = root / generation
++    for directory in (root, private_home):
++        directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
++        _private(directory, directory=True)
++    managed = private_home / 'agent-browser.json'
++    data = (json.dumps({'engine': 'chrome', 'executablePath': selected['executable'],
++                       'headed': False, 'args': ''}, sort_keys=True) + '\n').encode()
++    try:
++        fd = os.open(managed, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
++    except FileExistsError:
++        _private(managed)
++        if managed.read_bytes() != data:
++            raise BrowserPolicyError('browser_managed_config_changed')
++    else:
++        with os.fdopen(fd, 'wb') as stream:
++            stream.write(data)
++    # The explicit config disables both HOME and cwd config auto-discovery.
++    # Cwd/HOME also separate state, auth/encryption storage and relative outputs.
++    return {'PATH': '/usr/bin:/bin', 'LANG': 'C.UTF-8', 'HOME': str(private_home),
++            'XDG_CONFIG_HOME': str(private_home / '.config'),
++            'XDG_CACHE_HOME': str(private_home / '.cache'),
++            'XDG_DATA_HOME': str(private_home / '.local/share'),
++            'TMPDIR': socket_dir, 'AGENT_BROWSER_CONFIG': str(managed),
++            'AGENT_BROWSER_EXECUTABLE_PATH': selected['executable'],
++            'AGENT_BROWSER_SOCKET_DIR': socket_dir,
++            'AGENT_BROWSER_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS': str(idle_seconds * 1000)}

++def browser_task_key(task_id, *, cleanup=False):
++    """Namespace existing native dictionaries by retained principal<USER_HOME>
++
++    Raw model/native task IDs never select a different principal's cache entry.
++    Cleanup may use the same sticky capability after revocation, without re-admit.
++    """
++    cap = _CURRENT.get() if cleanup else current()
++    from hermes_constants import get_hermes_home
++    if cap is None:
++        from hermes_cli.browser_runtime import local_policy
++        if local_policy() is None:
++            return task_id
++        identity = [str(get_hermes_home()), 'operator']
++    else:
++        identity = [str(cap.authorization_home), str(cap.home), cap.key,
++                    cap.admission_generation, list(cap.directory_identity)]
++    prefix = 'friday-browser:' + _fingerprint(identity) + ':'
++    # Only internal cleanup receives already scoped keys. Public navigation and
++    # action lookups always hash the input, even if it resembles a session key.
++    if cleanup and str(task_id).startswith(prefix):
++        return task_id
++    return prefix + _fingerprint(str(task_id))

++def check_browser(session_info):
++    """Retained browser ownership; never replace a missing or revoked admission."""
++    from hermes_constants import get_hermes_home

++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        from hermes_cli.browser_runtime import local_policy
++        if local_policy() is not None:
++            raise ScopeDenied()

++    if home != str(get_hermes_home()) or (retained is not cap and not _same_scope(retained, cap)):
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    if retained is not None:
++        retained.check()
++

++def browser_revoked(session_info):
++    """Janitor uses the retained capability under its own home, never a new one."""
++    retained = session_info.get('_friday_user_scope')
++    if retained is None:
++        return False
++    try:
++        with authority(retained.home):
++            retained.check()
++        return False
++    except ScopeDenied:
++        return True

++    from tools import url_safety
++    rows = [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, '', ('93.184.216.34', 443))]
++    monkeypatch.setattr(url_safety, '_getaddrinfo', lambda *a: rows)
++    assert url_safety.is_safe_url('https://example.test', public_only=True)
++    rows.append((socket.AF_INET6, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, '', ('::ffff:127.0.0.1', 443, 0, 0)))
++    assert not url_safety.is_safe_url('https://example.test', public_only=True)

++@pytest.mark.parametrize('executable', [False, True])
++def test_pm_read_only_never_repairs_mode_or_facts(tmp_path, monkeypatch, executable):
++    import importlib
++    import pm
++    from pm import paths
++    install_module = importlib.import_module('pm.install')
++    home = tmp_path / 'home'; store = home / 'tools'
++    monkeypatch.setenv('HERMES_HOME', str(home))
++    monkeypatch.setenv('HERMES_RUNTIME_DIR', str(store))
++    package = pm.get_package('agent-browser'); target = pm.current_target()
++    lock = pm.Lockfile(paths.lockfile_path()); version = lock.version('agent-browser')
++    entry = store / package.store_entry(version, target)
++    binary = package.binary(entry, target)
++    if binary is None:
++        pytest.skip('donor target has no native agent-browser')
++    binary.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
++    binary.write_bytes(b'synthetic-file-not-a-program'); binary.chmod(0o700 if executable else 0o600)
++    facts = pm.Facts(store / 'facts.json')
++    facts.record('agent-browser', version, entry.name, {}, store,
++                 target=target, artifacts=[a['sha256'] for a in lock.artifacts('agent-browser', target)])
++    before = facts.path.read_bytes(), binary.stat().st_mode
++    monkeypatch.setattr(install_module, '_heal_exec_bit', lambda *a: pytest.fail('readiness mutated executable mode'))
++    selected = pm.installed_package('agent-browser', read_only=True)
++    assert (selected is not None) is executable
++    assert (facts.path.read_bytes(), binary.stat().st_mode) == before
++

++@pytest.fixture
++def managed_mechanics(configured, ordinary_host, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
++    """UNIT ONLY: inject missing admission to reach downstream source controls.
++
++    This fixture neither models an owner capability nor authorizes real launch.
++    No Popen, OS signal, pidfd, browser, service or network may execute here.

++    from tools import browser_tool_lifecycle as lifecycle, interrupt

++    monkeypatch.setattr(scope, '_ENGAGED', False)
++    token = scope._CURRENT.set(None)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(interrupt, 'is_interrupted', lambda: False)
++    for name in ('_active_sessions', '_last_active_session_key', '_session_last_activity',
++                 '_session_owner_homes', '_cleanup_failures', '_suspect_browser_sessions'):
++        monkeypatch.setattr(bt, name, {})

++    monkeypatch.setattr(bt, '_is_camofox_mode', lambda: pytest.fail('legacy driver lookup'))
++    real_popen = session._popen_agent_browser
++    monkeypatch.setattr(session, '_popen_agent_browser', lambda *a, **kw: pytest.fail('unconfigured process double'))
++    monkeypatch.setattr(lifecycle.os, 'pidfd_open', lambda *a: pytest.fail('real pidfd forbidden'))
++    monkeypatch.setattr(lifecycle.signal, 'pidfd_send_signal', lambda *a: pytest.fail('real signal forbidden'))
++    clock = SimpleNamespace(now=100.0)
++    timer = SimpleNamespace(monotonic=lambda: clock.now)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'time', timer)
++    # Keep wall-clock access needed by donor output/logging paths.
++    import time
++    monkeypatch.setattr(session, 'time', SimpleNamespace(monotonic=lambda: clock.now, time=time.time))
++    deadline_token = runtime._BROWSER_DEADLINE.set(110.0)
++    home = tmp_path / 'home'; home.mkdir(mode=0o700)
++    # UNIT ONLY compatibility seam for the added owner/registry association.
++    # Dedicated test_browser_owner_join.py checks the real association methods;
++    # these older mechanics controls still prove only retained client behavior.
++    from tools import browser_tool_owner as owner_module, process_registry as registry_module
++    from contextlib import nullcontext
++    injected_owner = SimpleNamespace(check=lambda: 110.0, original_deadline=110.0,
++        fence=lambda deadline=None: nullcontext(110.0), require_route_task=lambda value: None)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(owner_module, 'time', timer)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(owner_module, 'current_browser_owner', lambda: injected_owner)
++    def injected_bind(key, info):
++        info['_friday_execution_owner'] = injected_owner
++        return injected_owner
++    monkeypatch.setattr(owner_module, 'bind_browser_session', injected_bind)
++    class RegistryDouble:
++        def prepare_browser_client(self, owner, key, info, custody):
++            assert owner is injected_owner and info.get('_friday_direct_client') is None
++            record = info.setdefault('_friday_registry_session', SimpleNamespace(process=None, stopped=False, web_deadline=110.0, browser_client_deadline=custody['deadline']))
++            record.custody = custody
++            return record
++        def retain_browser_client(self, record, info, custody, proc):
++            assert info['_friday_direct_client'] is custody and custody['proc'] is proc
++            record.process = proc
++        def confirm_browser_client_wait(self, record, info, custody, proc, rc):
++            assert record.process is proc and custody['wait_confirmed'] and rc == proc.returncode
++            record.wait_confirmed = True
++        def latch_browser_stop(self, record):
++            record.stopped = True
++    monkeypatch.setattr(registry_module, 'process_registry', RegistryDouble())
++    try:
++        with scope.authority(home):
++            key = scope.browser_task_key('task')
++            info = session._get_session_info(key, deadline=110.0)
++            yield SimpleNamespace(bt=bt, session=session, lifecycle=lifecycle, key=key, info=info,
++                                  clock=clock, home=home, tmp=tmp_path, real_popen=real_popen)

++def _fake_client(monkeypatch, mechanics, on_wait=None):
++    events = []
++    class Client:
++        returncode = 0
++        pid = 987654321
++        def kill(self):
++            events.append('kill')
++        def wait(self, timeout=None):
++            events.append(('wait', timeout))
++            if on_wait and 'kill' not in events:
++                on_wait()
++            return self.returncode
++    client = Client()
++    def popen(argv, **kwargs):
++        # Use the production _popen_agent_browser wrapper; only the actual OS
++        # creation is replaced. Its pre-published record must already exist.
++        events.append(('spawn', mechanics.info['_friday_direct_client']['deadline']))
++        os.write(kwargs['stdout'], b'{"success":true,"data":{}}')
++        os.write(kwargs['stderr'], b'client diagnostic')
++        return client
++    monkeypatch.setattr(mechanics.session, '_popen_agent_browser', mechanics.real_popen)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(mechanics.session.subprocess, 'Popen', popen)
++    return client, events
++
++
++@pytest.mark.parametrize('failure', ['revoked', 'runtime', 'interrupt'])
++def test_post_popen_refusal_stops_and_waits_despite_unconfirmed_teardown(managed_mechanics, monkeypatch, failure):
++    m = managed_mechanics
++    from tools import interrupt
++    def on_wait():
++        if failure == 'revoked':
++            m.info['_friday_stop_unconfirmed'] = True
++        elif failure == 'runtime':
++            raise RuntimeError('teardown exploded')
++        else:
++            monkeypatch.setattr(interrupt, 'is_interrupted', lambda: True)
++    _, events = _fake_client(monkeypatch, m, on_wait)
++    # Actual dispatch -> spawn/collect -> authority/interrupt -> real release
++    # sees no published daemon identity and therefore retains STOP_UNCONFIRMED.
++    _, result = m.session._dispatch_browser_command(m.key, m.info, '/owned/cli', 'open',
++                   ['https://example.test'], 10, None, deadline=110.0)
++    assert result['success'] is False and result['custody'] == 'STOP_UNCONFIRMED'
++    if failure == 'runtime':  # failure before an actual wait result
++        assert 'kill' in events and events[events.index('kill') + 1][0] == 'wait'
++    else:
++        assert 'kill' not in events  # exact child already returned from wait
++    assert 'STOP_UNCONFIRMED' in result['custody']
++    if failure == 'runtime':
++        assert 'teardown exploded' in result['error']
++    elif failure == 'interrupt':
++        assert 'browser_interrupted' in result['error']
++    assert m.bt._active_sessions[m.key] is m.info
++    diagnostic = m.info['_friday_client_diagnostics']
++    assert diagnostic['wait_confirmed']
++    assert all(Path(path).exists() for path in diagnostic['output_paths'])
++    assert 'client diagnostic' not in json.dumps(result)
++    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
++        m.session._get_session_info(m.key, deadline=110.0)
++    assert sum(isinstance(e, tuple) and e[0] == 'spawn' for e in events) == 1
++
++
++def test_client_wait_is_attempted_even_when_client_kill_raises(managed_mechanics, monkeypatch):
++    m = managed_mechanics

++@pytest.mark.parametrize('stage', ['fence', 'preparation', 'retry'])
++def test_absolute_deadline_reaches_real_spawn_without_replenishment(managed_mechanics, monkeypatch, stage):
++    m = managed_mechanics
++    _, events = _fake_client(monkeypatch, m)
++    if stage == 'fence':
++        original = m.session._check_session_authority
++        def delayed(key, info):
++            owner = original(key, info)
++            m.clock.now = 111.0
++            return owner
++        monkeypatch.setattr(m.session, '_check_session_authority', delayed)
++        with pytest.raises(runtime.BrowserPolicyError, match='deadline_exhausted'):
++            m.session.run_fenced_pair(m.info, lambda: m.session._dispatch_browser_command(
++                m.key, m.info, '/owned/cli', 'snapshot', [], 10, None, deadline=110.0), deadline=110.0)
++    elif stage == 'preparation':
++        original = m.session._agent_browser_command_env
++        def delayed(*args, **kwargs):
++            env = original(*args, **kwargs)
++            m.clock.now = 111.0
++            return env
++        monkeypatch.setattr(m.session, '_agent_browser_command_env', delayed)
++        _, result = m.session._dispatch_browser_command(m.key, m.info, '/owned/cli', 'snapshot', [], 10, None, deadline=110.0)
++        assert result['success'] is False and 'deadline_exhausted' in result['error']
++    else:
++        # A protocol error reaches the real dispatcher/spawn. Cleanup is not
++        # declared successful: time expiring there must not grant a retry.
++        def popen(argv, **kwargs):
++            events.append(('spawn', m.info['_friday_direct_client']['deadline']))
++            os.write(kwargs['stdout'], b'not-json')
++            return SimpleNamespace(pid=987654321, returncode=101, wait=lambda **kw: 101, kill=lambda: None)
++        monkeypatch.setattr(m.session.subprocess, 'Popen', popen)
++        def refused_teardown(*args):
++            m.clock.now = 111.0
++            m.info['_friday_stop_unconfirmed'] = True
++            raise RuntimeError('STOP_UNCONFIRMED')
++        monkeypatch.setattr(m.lifecycle, '_force_reap_browser_session', refused_teardown)
++        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='STOP_UNCONFIRMED'):
++            m.session._run_browser_command(m.key, 'snapshot', timeout=10)
++        assert events == [('spawn', 110.0)]
++        return
++    assert not any(isinstance(e, tuple) and e[0] == 'spawn' for e in events)
++

++@pytest.mark.parametrize('observation', ['dead-leader', 'zombie-leader', 'child-alive', 'enumeration-denied', 'signal-denied', 'listener-alive'])
++def test_no_leader_or_signal_observation_can_release_managed_custody(managed_mechanics, monkeypatch, observation):
++    m = managed_mechanics
++    folder = Path(m.session._prepare_session_socket_dir(m.info['session_name'], deadline=110.0))
++    (folder / (m.info['session_name'] + '.pid')).write_text('999999')
++    m.info['_friday_launch_attempted'] = True
++    from gateway import status
++    m.info['_friday_daemon_identity'] = (999999, 'original-start', 42)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(status, 'get_process_start_time', lambda pid:
++                        None if observation in ('dead-leader', 'zombie-leader') else 'original-start')
++    def signal_only(fd, sig):
++        if observation == 'signal-denied':
++            raise PermissionError('signal denied')
++    monkeypatch.setattr(m.lifecycle.signal, 'pidfd_send_signal', signal_only)
++    # There is deliberately no joined owner API that could establish whether
++    # descendants/listeners survive. Neither leader liveness nor a signal is it.
++    monkeypatch.setattr(m.lifecycle, '_pid_exists', lambda pid: observation not in ('dead-leader', 'zombie-leader'))
++    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='owned_scope_proof_required'):
++        m.lifecycle._release_session_resources(m.key, m.info)
++    assert m.bt._active_sessions[m.key] is m.info
++    assert (folder / '.friday-custody').exists()
++    # The existing orphan sweep must not delete this unresolved marker either.
++    assert m.lifecycle._reap_socket_dir(str(folder), m.info['session_name'], set()) is False
++    assert folder.exists()
++

++    signals = []

++    monkeypatch.setattr(m.lifecycle.signal, 'pidfd_send_signal', lambda fd, sig: signals.append((fd, sig)))

++        m.lifecycle._release_session_resources(m.key, m.info)

++@pytest.mark.parametrize('condition', ['replacement', 'unrelated', 'identity-denied'])
++def test_identity_capture_closes_handle_without_adopting_unverified_generation(managed_mechanics, monkeypatch, condition):
++    m = managed_mechanics
++    from gateway import status

++    class Observed:
++        def name(self):
++            if condition == 'identity-denied':
++                raise psutil.AccessDenied(999999)
++            return 'unrelated' if condition == 'unrelated' else 'agent-browser'
++        def cmdline(self):
++            return ['unrelated'] if condition == 'unrelated' else ['agent-browser', str(folder)]

++            return {}

++    closed = []

++        m.lifecycle._retain_managed_daemon_identity(m.info, str(folder))

++        pytest.fail('browser effect before required admission')

++    monkeypatch.setattr(subprocess, 'Popen', forbidden)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(subprocess, 'run', forbidden)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_cdp_tool, '_WS_AVAILABLE', True)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_cdp_tool, 'websockets', SimpleNamespace(connect=forbidden))
++    # A real-looking already retained supervisor cannot grant execution.
++    retained = SimpleNamespace(respond_to_dialog=forbidden, evaluate=forbidden, call=forbidden)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_supervisor.SUPERVISOR_REGISTRY, 'get', lambda *a: retained)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_supervisor.SUPERVISOR_REGISTRY, 'get_or_start', forbidden)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_control_broker, 'get_browser_control_broker', forbidden)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_use_cli, '_find_cli', forbidden)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(browser_use_cli, '_workspace_dir', forbidden)
++    yield blocked_dispatch[0], modules, forbidden
++    assert touched == []

++# BHR2: real session/Popen-wrapper paths with an OS-creation double only.
++@pytest.mark.parametrize('kill_fails,wait_failure,expired', [
++    (True, 'denied', False), (False, 'delayed', False),
++    (True, 'delayed', True), (False, 'delayed', True),
++])
++def test_unconfirmed_client_retained_then_same_owner_wait_collects(
++        managed_mechanics, monkeypatch, kill_fails, wait_failure, expired):
++    m = managed_mechanics
++    original = PermissionError('original in-flight refusal')
++    client, events = _fake_client(monkeypatch, m)
++    client.returncode = None
++    state = {'first': True, 'collect': False}
++    waits = []
++    def wait(timeout=None):
++        waits.append(timeout)
++        if state['first']:
++            state['first'] = False
++            if expired:
++                m.clock.now = 111.0
++            raise original
++        if not state['collect']:
++            if wait_failure == 'denied':
++                raise PermissionError('wait denied')
++            raise m.session.subprocess.TimeoutExpired('original child', timeout)
++        client.returncode = -9
++        return -9
++    def kill():
++        events.append('kill')
++        if kill_fails:
++            raise PermissionError('kill denied')
++    monkeypatch.setattr(client, 'wait', wait)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(client, 'kill', kill)
++    with pytest.raises(PermissionError) as error:
++        m.session._spawn_and_collect(m.key, m.info, ['/owned/cli', 'snapshot'], 'snapshot', 'chrome', 10,
++                                    deadline=110.0)
++    assert error.value is original
++    assert any('STOP_UNCONFIRMED' in note for note in original.__notes__)
++    pending = m.info['_friday_direct_client']
++    assert pending['proc'] is client and pending['pid'] == client.pid
++    assert pending['deadline'] == 110.0 and not pending['wait_confirmed']
++    assert pending['task_id'] == m.key and m.bt._active_sessions[m.key] is m.info
++    assert 'client_wait' in ';'.join(pending['errors'])
++    assert all(Path(path).exists() for path in pending['output_paths'])
++    assert 'client diagnostic' in Path(pending['output_paths'][1]).read_text()
++    assert waits[-1] == (0.0 if expired else 1.0)
++    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
++        m.session._get_session_info(m.key, deadline=110.0)
++    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='actual_client_wait_required'):
++        m.session._release_waited_managed_client(m.key, m.info)
++    assert m.info['_friday_direct_client']['proc'] is client
++    # A daemon publishing after the failure cannot substitute a newly observed
++    # identity or make the original client disappear from existing custody.
++    socket_dir = Path(pending['output_paths'][0]).parent
++    (socket_dir / (m.info['session_name'] + '.pid')).write_text('123456789')
++    monkeypatch.setattr(m.lifecycle, '_read_pid_file', lambda *a: pytest.fail('late identity adoption'))
++    with pytest.raises(PermissionError, match='stop_unconfirmed'):
++        m.lifecycle._retain_managed_daemon_identity(m.info, str(socket_dir))
++    state['collect'] = True
++    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='owned_scope_proof_required'):
++        m.lifecycle._force_reap_browser_session(m.key)
++    assert '_friday_direct_client' not in m.info  # only now: actual same-child wait
++    diagnostic = m.info['_friday_client_diagnostics']
++    assert diagnostic['wait_confirmed'] and diagnostic['returncode'] == -9
++    assert diagnostic['pid'] == client.pid and diagnostic['deadline'] == 110.0
++    assert all(Path(path).exists() for path in diagnostic['output_paths'])
++    assert m.info['_friday_stop_unconfirmed'] and m.bt._active_sessions[m.key] is m.info
++    assert '_friday_daemon_identity' not in m.info
++    assert sum(isinstance(event, tuple) and event[0] == 'spawn' for event in events) == 1
++    if expired:
++        assert waits[1:] == [0.0, 0.0]  # no replenished cleanup budget
++
++
++def test_client_handle_published_before_fd_close_and_no_false_wait_release(managed_mechanics, monkeypatch):
++    m = managed_mechanics
++    client, events = _fake_client(monkeypatch, m)
++    close = m.session.os.close
++    observed = []
++    def observe_close(fd):
++        retained = m.info['_friday_direct_client']
++        assert retained['proc'] is client and retained['pid'] == client.pid
++        assert retained['deadline'] == 110.0 and not retained['wait_confirmed']
++        observed.append(fd)
++        close(fd)
++        raise OSError('injected close failure after close')
++    monkeypatch.setattr(m.session.os, 'close', observe_close)
++    proc = m.real_popen(['/owned/cli'], {'HOME': str(m.home)}, str(m.tmp), 'snapshot',
++                        deadline=110.0, task_id=m.key, session_info=m.info)
++    assert proc is client and len(observed) == 2
++    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='actual_client_wait_required'):
++        m.session._release_waited_managed_client(m.key, m.info)
++    assert m.info['_friday_direct_client']['proc'] is client
++    assert not any(isinstance(event, tuple) and event[0] == 'wait' for event in events)

++def test_legacy_public_api_and_async_signature_preserved_without_policy(monkeypatch):
++    import asyncio
++    import inspect
++    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'local_policy', lambda: None)
++    calls = []
++    def donor(value, *, optional=3):
++        calls.append((value, optional))
++        return value + optional
++    async def async_donor(value, timeout=5):
++        return value
++    wrapped = runtime.browser_action_entry(donor)
++    async_wrapped = runtime.browser_action_entry(async_donor)
++    assert inspect.signature(wrapped) == inspect.signature(donor)
++    assert inspect.signature(async_wrapped) == inspect.signature(async_donor)
++    assert wrapped(2, optional=4) == 6 and calls == [(2, 4)]
++    assert asyncio.run(async_wrapped(7)) == 7

++"""Control SOURCES only: require a separate finite guard admission before running.
++
++Actual association/registry methods and actual native WebCall/UserScope classes
++are used. Authority storage, checkpoint location, process handles and network
++backend are unit doubles. No browser/cgroup/network/production acceptance is
++asserted, and the real production admission remains an unconditional refusal.
++"""
++import contextvars
++import json

++@pytest.fixture
++def native_owner(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
++    clock = SimpleNamespace(now=100.0)
++    timer = SimpleNamespace(monotonic=lambda: clock.now)
++    for module in (owners, calls, web, urls):
++        monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'time', timer)
++    from tools import interrupt
++    monkeypatch.setattr(interrupt, 'is_interrupted', lambda: False)
++    home = tmp_path / 'profile'; home.mkdir(mode=0o700)
++    st = home.stat()
++    cap = users.UserScope(tmp_path / 'authority', home, 'alice',
++        ('telegram', 'botprofile', 'bot-id', 'alice'), {'runtime_profile': 'alice'},
++        (st.st_dev, st.st_ino), 3)
++    def checked(value):
++        if value.revoked:
++            raise users.ScopeDenied()
++        return value
++    # Only persistent authority lookup is replaced; owner snapshot/current
++    # ContextVars and generation/principal/home comparisons are production code.
++    monkeypatch.setattr(users.UserScope, 'check', checked)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(users, '_ENGAGED', False)
++    token = users._CURRENT.set(cap)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(users, 'check_browser', lambda info: checked(info['_friday_user_scope']))
++    agent = SimpleNamespace(session_id='conversation-A', _current_turn_id='turn-A',
++                            _web_run_deadline=110.0, _web_turn=calls.WebTurn())
++    call = calls.make_web_call(agent, 120.0, 'task-A', 'call-A')
++    call.session_key = 'conversation-key-A'
++    try:
++        with users.authority(home), calls.bind_web_call(call):
++            owner = owners.current_browser_owner()
++            yield SimpleNamespace(clock=clock, cap=cap, owner=owner, call=call,
++                                  agent=agent, home=home, tmp=tmp_path)
++    finally:
++        users._CURRENT.reset(token)
++

++def _info(n):
++    return {'session_name': 'native-random-A', '_friday_managed': True,
++            '_friday_owner_home': str(n.home), '_friday_user_scope': n.cap}
++

++class Registry(web.WebProcessMixin):
++    """Exercise real association/checkpoint verification, using a private test store."""
++    _new_session = staticmethod(processes.ProcessRegistry._new_session)
++
++    def __init__(self, checkpoint):
++        self._lock = threading.RLock()
++        self._running = {}
++        self.checkpoint = checkpoint
++        self.writes = 0
++
++    def _write_checkpoint(self):
++        rows = [{**{key: getattr(s, key) for key in processes._CHECKPOINT_FIELDS},
++                 'session_id': s.id} for s in self._running.values()]
++        self.checkpoint.write_text(json.dumps(rows))
++        self.writes += 1
++
++    @staticmethod
++    def _safe_host_start_time(pid):
++        return 123456  # Unit birth observation; never a live kernel claim.
++

++def _answer(ip):
++    family = socket.AF_INET6 if ':' in ip else socket.AF_INET
++    return (family, socket.SOCK_STREAM, socket.IPPROTO_TCP, '', (ip, 443))

++@pytest.mark.parametrize('corruption', ['missing', 'relaxed_to_owner_deadline'])
++def test_checkpoint_command_deadline_corruption_keeps_original_client(native_owner, registry, monkeypatch, corruption):
++    info, custody, session = _prepare_unit_only(native_owner, registry, monkeypatch)
++    # A valid retained owner limit does not replace a tighter command limit.
++    custody['deadline'] = session.browser_client_deadline = 104.0
++    assert session.web_deadline == 110.0
++    original_writer = registry._write_checkpoint
++
++    def corrupt_writer():
++        original_writer()
++        rows = json.loads(registry.checkpoint.read_text())
++        if corruption == 'missing':
++            rows[0].pop('browser_client_deadline')
++        else:
++            rows[0]['browser_client_deadline'] = session.web_deadline
++        registry.checkpoint.write_text(json.dumps(rows))
++
++    monkeypatch.setattr(registry, '_write_checkpoint', corrupt_writer)
++    proc = SimpleNamespace(pid=12345678, returncode=None)
++    custody.update(proc=proc, pid=proc.pid)
++    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='web_checkpoint_required'):
++        registry.retain_browser_client(session, info, custody, proc)
++    assert registry._running[session.id] is session and session.process is proc
++    assert session.browser_client_custody is custody
++    assert info['_friday_direct_client']['proc'] is proc
++    assert session.browser_client_deadline == custody['deadline'] == 104.0
++    assert not session.browser_client_wait_confirmed and not session.web_settled
++

++def test_launch_stop_original_inverted_interleaving_is_bounded(joined_client, monkeypatch):
++    """Separate finite guard required; old cleanup->turn bytes fail this schedule.
++
++    Pause the real owner's call precheck before its turn acquisition, after the
++    launcher has passed the initial slot check. Stop owns the actual turn while
++    entering the actual registry/lifecycle cleanup path. Fixed launch has not
++    acquired cleanup yet, so stop can finish and launch must refuse.
++    """
++    import inspect
++    j = joined_client; n = j.n
++    custody = {'output_paths': (), 'deadline': 110.0}
++    record = j.registry.prepare_browser_client(n.owner, j.key, j.info, custody)
++    armed = threading.Event(); prechecked = threading.Event(); turn_held = threading.Event()
++    release = threading.Event(); outcomes = []; created = []
++    original_slot = j.sessions._require_managed_client_slot
++    def slot(*args):
++        original_slot(*args)
++        armed.set()
++    monkeypatch.setattr(j.sessions, '_require_managed_client_slot', slot)
++    original_current = calls.current_web_call
++    def paused_current():
++        call = original_current()
++        frame = inspect.currentframe().f_back
++        if (threading.current_thread().name == 'browser-launch-control' and armed.is_set()
++                and frame.f_code.co_name == 'check' and frame.f_globals.get('__name__') == owners.__name__
++                and not prechecked.is_set()):
++            prechecked.set()
++            assert release.wait(2), 'control release missing'
++        return call
++    monkeypatch.setattr(calls, 'current_web_call', paused_current)
++    monkeypatch.setattr(j.sessions.subprocess, 'Popen', lambda *a, **kw: created.append(a))
++    context = contextvars.copy_context()
++    def launch():
++        try:
++            context.run(j.sessions._popen_agent_browser, ['unit-only'], {'HOME': str(n.home)},
++                str(n.tmp), 'race', deadline=110., managed_required=True, task_id=j.key, session_info=j.info)
++        except (PermissionError, RuntimeError, TimeoutError) as exc:
++            outcomes.append(('launch-refused', type(exc).__name__))
++    def stop():
++        with n.call.turn.lock:
++            turn_held.set()
++            outcomes.append(('stop', n.call.stop()['status']))
++    launcher = threading.Thread(target=launch, name='browser-launch-control')
++    stopper = threading.Thread(target=stop, name='browser-stop-control')
++    launcher.start()
++    try:
++        assert prechecked.wait(2), 'launch did not reach actual pre-turn seam'
++        stopper.start()
++        assert turn_held.wait(2), 'stop did not retain actual turn fence'
++    finally:
++        release.set()
++    launcher.join(3); stopper.join(3)
++    assert not launcher.is_alive() and not stopper.is_alive(), 'cleanup/turn ABBA cycle'
++    assert ('stop', 'STOP_UNCONFIRMED') in outcomes
++    assert any(item[0] == 'launch-refused' for item in outcomes)
++    assert not created and record.web_stop_requested and j.registry._running[record.id] is record
++
++
++def test_stop_during_returned_popen_keeps_exact_handle_and_intent(joined_client, monkeypatch):
++    j = joined_client; n = j.n
++    proc = SimpleNamespace(pid=12345678, returncode=None)
++    def returned(*a, **kw):

++"""Small association to the caller already owned by Hermes, never an admission token.
++
++The native executor creates WebCall/WebTurn. This module retains those actual
++objects, the actual UserScope and its original deadline; it creates no job,
++clock, process manager, network service or authority from configuration.
++"""
++from contextlib import contextmanager
++from dataclasses import dataclass
++import math
++from pathlib import Path
++import time
++
++
++class BrowserOwnerRefused(PermissionError):
++    pass
++
++
++def _call_identity(call):
++    values = tuple(getattr(call, k, None) for k in
++                   ('task_id', 'session_id', 'session_key', 'turn_id', 'call_id'))
++    if any(type(v) is not str or not v or len(v) > 4096 for v in values):
++        raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_actual_native_call_identity_required')
++    return values
++
++
++def _scope_identity(cap):
++    from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import _fingerprint
++    return (str(cap.authorization_home), str(cap.home), cap.profile,
++            tuple(cap.principal), cap.admission_generation,
++            tuple(cap.directory_identity), _fingerprint(cap.binding))
++
++
++def _finite_deadline(value):
++    if type(value) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(value):
++        raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_original_owner_deadline_required')
++    if time.monotonic() >= value:
++        raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_original_owner_deadline_exhausted')
++    return value
++
++
++@contextmanager
++def browser_lock(lock, deadline):
++    """Bound an existing lock by an already retained ceiling; never renew it."""
++    if type(deadline) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(deadline):
++        raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_original_owner_deadline_required')
++    if not lock.acquire(timeout=max(0.0, min(2.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))):
++        raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_owner_lock_unsettled')
++    try:
++        yield
++    finally:
++        lock.release()
++
++
++@dataclass(frozen=True)
++class BrowserOwner:
++    call: object
++    scope: object
++    call_identity: tuple
++    scope_identity: tuple
++    original_deadline: float
++    boot_id: str
++
++    def check(self):
++        """Recheck actual native objects; a subsequent call cannot extend the owner clock."""
++        from agent.web_call_context import current_web_call, WebCall
++        from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import current, _same_scope
++        from hermes_constants import get_hermes_home
++        actual = current_web_call()
++        if (type(self.call) is not WebCall or type(actual) is not WebCall
++                or _call_identity(self.call) != self.call_identity
++                or _call_identity(actual)[:4] != self.call_identity[:4]
++                or actual.turn is not self.call.turn):
++            raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_native_owner_changed')
++        with browser_lock(self.call.turn.lock, min(self.original_deadline, actual.deadline(),
++                getattr(self.call.turn, 'browser_deadline', self.original_deadline))):
++            if (self.call not in self.call.turn.calls or actual not in self.call.turn.calls
++                    or self.call.stopped.is_set() or self.call.turn.stopped.is_set()):
++                raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_original_owner_stopped')
++            self.call.check()
++            actual.check()
++        cap = current(required=True)
++        if (not _same_scope(cap, self.scope) or _scope_identity(cap) != self.scope_identity
++                or _scope_identity(self.scope) != self.scope_identity
++                or str(get_hermes_home()) != self.scope_identity[1]):
++            raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_principal_profile_generation_changed')
++        self.scope.check()
++        if Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip() != self.boot_id:
++            raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_original_boot_changed')
++        return _finite_deadline(min(self.original_deadline, actual.deadline()))
++
++    @contextmanager
++    def fence(self, deadline=None):
++        """Launch/publication lock order is turn -> cleanup -> registry.
++
++        Stop uses the same turn fence. Hold it until the returned child and its
++        checkpoint custody have been published, including error paths.
++        """
++        ceiling = self.original_deadline if deadline is None else min(self.original_deadline, deadline)
++        ceiling = min(ceiling, getattr(self.call.turn, 'browser_deadline', ceiling))
++        with browser_lock(self.call.turn.lock, ceiling):
++            ceiling = min(ceiling, self.check())
++            yield ceiling
++
++    def task_key(self):
++        self.check()
++        from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import browser_task_key
++        return browser_task_key(self.call_identity[0])
++
++    def require_route_task(self, task_id):
++        if task_id is not None and task_id not in (self.call_identity[0], self.task_key()):
++            raise BrowserOwnerRefused('browser_foreign_route_task')
++        self.check()
++
++    def public_http_backend(self, schemes_by_origin_var, *, is_async=False):
++        """Reuse the installed HTTP backend. It is not the missing Rust/Chrome route."""
++        self.check()
++        from tools.url_safety import _SSRFGuardedNetworkBackend, _SSRFGuardedAsyncNetworkBackend
++        cls = _SSRFGuardedAsyncNetworkBackend if is_async else _SSRFGuardedNetworkBackend
++        return cls(schemes_by_origin_var, public_only=True, owner_check=self.check)
++
++    def identity(self):
++        """Non-secret fields persisted in the existing ProcessRegistry checkpoint."""
++        return {'task_id': self.call_identity[0], 'parent_session_id': self.call_identity[1],
++                'session_key': self.call_identity[2], 'turn_id': self.call_identity[3],
++                'first_call_id': self.call_identity[4], 'authorization_home': self.scope_identity[0],
++                'profile_home': self.scope_identity[1], 'profile': self.scope_identity[2],
++                'principal': list(self.scope_identity[3]), 'access_generation': self.scope_identity[4],
++                'directory_identity': list(self.scope_identity[5]), 'binding_sha256': self.scope_identity[6],
++                'original_deadline': self.original_deadline, 'boot_id': self.boot_id}
++
++
++def current_browser_owner():

++def public_web_connect_ips(host: str, port: int, scheme: str) -> list[str]:
++    """Finite direct HTTP(S) dial policy, NOT a Chromium page-network grant.
++
++    Validate every answer before returning the donor's capped dial list. No
++    global private/fake-IP setting or ambient proxy can authorize a destination.
++    Native redirects/subframes/fetch/WS/service-worker still need an actual
++    owned network boundary; this function cannot enforce their traffic.
++    """
++    if (type(host) is not str or not host or any(ord(c) <= 32 for c in host)
++            or '%' in host or type(port) is not int or not 1 <= port <= 65535
++            or scheme not in _HTTP_SCHEMES):
++        raise SSRFConnectionBlocked('public_web_dial_identity_invalid')
++    hostname = _normalize_hostname(host)
++    if not hostname or hostname in _BLOCKED_HOSTNAMES:
++        raise SSRFConnectionBlocked('public_web_internal_hostname')
++    try:
++        answers = _getaddrinfo(hostname, port)
++        safe_ips = []
++        for raw, ip_str, ip in _iter_resolved_ips(answers):
++            if (ip is None or '%' in raw or _is_always_blocked_ip(ip)
++                    or _is_blocked_ip(ip) or not _embedded_ipv4(ip).is_global):
++                raise SSRFConnectionBlocked('public_web_nonpublic_or_unknown_answer')
++            if ip_str not in safe_ips and len(safe_ips) < _MAX_SSRF_CONNECT_IPS:
++                safe_ips.append(ip_str)
++    except SSRFConnectionBlocked:
++        raise
++    except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
++        raise SSRFConnectionBlocked('public_web_resolution_failed') from None
++    if not safe_ips:
++        raise SSRFConnectionBlocked('public_web_resolution_empty')
++    return safe_ips

+ class _SSRFGuardedBackendBase:
+     """httpcore backend that re-resolves + validates at connect time and dials a vetted IP.
+     Host/SNI stay on the original hostname; Unix sockets are refused outright. Candidate IPs are

+

++    def _dial_timeout(self, timeout):
++        if self._owner_check is None:
++            return timeout
++        deadline = self._owner_check()
++        if type(deadline) not in (float, int) or not math.isfinite(deadline):

++            raise SSRFConnectionBlocked('public_web_finite_dial_timeout_required')

++"""Bind native persisted prompt reuse to the existing ordinary admission.
++
++Provenance lives in the session's existing model_config, beside native lifecycle
++metadata; the native prompt writer stores both in one transaction. It is not
++an authorization grant, and unscoped donor sessions retain their behavior.
++"""
++import hashlib
++import json
++from pathlib import Path
++from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import current, check_agent, check_database, ScopeDenied
++
++FIELD = "_friday_prompt_scope"
++
++
++def _store(cap, db):
++    if db is None or Path(db.db_path) != cap.home / "state.db":
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    check_database(cap)
++
++
++def _stamp(cap, session_id, prompt):
++    return {"schema": "friday.prompt-scope.v1", "principal_id": cap.key,
++            "generation": cap.admission_generation, "home": str(cap.home),
++            "directory_identity": list(cap.directory_identity), "session_id": session_id,
++            "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()}
++
++
++def matches_provenance(stored, prompt, admission):
++    """Validate the complete stored stamp against this admission and its prompt.
++
++    The admission may describe a newly built prompt. Recompute only its digest
++    for the stored bytes; every other field, including the key set, must match.
++    Restore and atomic tool-pin invalidation use this same predicate.
++    """
++    if not isinstance(prompt, str) or not prompt:
++        return False
++    expected = {**admission, "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()}
++    return isinstance(stored, dict) and stored == expected
++
++
++def check_store(agent):
++    cap = current()
++    if cap is not None:
++        check_agent(agent)
++        if agent._session_db is not None:
++            _store(cap, agent._session_db)
++
++
++def for_persistence(db, session_id, prompt):
++    cap = current()
++    if cap is None:
++        return None
++    _store(cap, db)
++    result = _stamp(cap, session_id, prompt) if isinstance(prompt, str) else None
++    cap.check()
++    return result
++
++
++def matches(agent, prompt, session_row):
++    cap = current()
++    if cap is None:
++        return True
++    check_store(agent)
++    try:
++        config = json.loads((session_row or {}).get("model_config") or "{}")
++        stored = config.get(FIELD) if isinstance(config, dict) else None
++    except (ValueError, TypeError):
++        stored = None
++    matches = isinstance(prompt, str) and matches_provenance(stored, prompt, _stamp(cap, agent.session_id, prompt))
++    cap.check()
++    return matches
