++"""Bounded descriptor I/O for native skills under the existing UserScope.
++
++No identity or skill store is introduced. The native tree, usage and ledger
++formats are retained. A transaction stages the native tree in memory and
++publishes it with one same-parent Linux renameat2; unsupported hosts refuse.
++"""
++from contextlib import contextmanager
++from contextvars import ContextVar
++import ctypes
++import hashlib
++import os
++from pathlib import Path
++import stat
++import uuid
++
++from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import current, ScopeDenied
++from hermes_cli.skill_lock import require_skill_lock
++
++MAX_FILE = 1048576
++MAX_STATE = 8 * MAX_FILE
++MAX_TREE = 32 * MAX_FILE
++MAX_ENTRIES = 4096
++MAX_DEPTH = 16
++_TX = ContextVar('friday_native_skill_transaction', default=None)
++
++
++def active():
++    return current()
++
++
++def authority_stamp():
++    cap = current(required=True)
++    return {'principal_id': cap.key, 'generation': cap.admission_generation,
++            'home': str(cap.home), 'directory_identity': list(cap.directory_identity)}
++
++
++def check_stamp(value):
++    if value != authority_stamp():
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++
++
++def _parts(path, cap):
++    p = Path(path)
++    if not p.is_absolute() or '\\' in str(p) or '\x00' in str(p) or '..' in p.parts:
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    try:
++        parts = p.relative_to(cap.home).parts
++    except ValueError:
++        raise ScopeDenied() from None
++    if not parts or len(parts) > MAX_DEPTH:
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    if not (parts[0] == 'skills' or parts[:2] == ('.curator_backups', 'blobs')
++            or parts[:2] == ('pending', 'skills') or parts == ('.skills.lock',)):
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    return parts
++
++
++def _private(st, directory=False):
++    if (st.st_uid != os.getuid() or st.st_mode & 0o077
++            or (directory and not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode))
++            or (not directory and (not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_nlink != 1))):
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++
++
++def _identity(st):
++    return st.st_dev, st.st_ino
++
++
++@contextmanager
++def _directory(path, *, create=False):
++    cap = current(required=True)
++    path = Path(path)
++    if path != cap.home:
++        _parts(path, cap)
++    fds = [os.open('/', os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)]
++    links = []
++    try:
++        partial = Path('/')
++        for name in path.parts[1:]:
++            partial /= name
++            parent = fds[-1]
++            try:
++                child = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent)
++            except FileNotFoundError:
++                if not create or not partial.is_relative_to(cap.home) or partial == cap.home:
++                    raise
++                cap.check()
++                os.mkdir(name, 0o700, dir_fd=parent)
++                child = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent)
++            st = os.fstat(child)
++            fds.append(child)
++            links.append((parent, name, _identity(st), partial.is_relative_to(cap.home)))
++            if partial.is_relative_to(cap.home):
++                _private(st, True)
++            if partial == cap.home and _identity(st) != cap.directory_identity:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++        def check():
++            cap.check()
++            for parent, name, identity, private in links:
++                st = os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
++                if not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) or _identity(st) != identity:
++                    raise ScopeDenied()
++                if private:
++                    _private(st, True)
++        check()
++        yield fds[-1], check
++        check()
++    except FileNotFoundError:
++        raise
++    except (OSError, ValueError):
++        raise ScopeDenied() from None
++    finally:
++        for fd in reversed(fds):
++            os.close(fd)
++
++
++def _limit(path):
++    # A native20-op approval batch can contain both old/new100k-char fields.
++    cap = current(required=True)
++    if Path(path).is_relative_to(cap.home / 'pending' / 'skills'):
++        return MAX_TREE
++    return MAX_STATE if Path(path).name in {'.curator_ledger.jsonl', '.usage.json'} else MAX_FILE
++
++
++def _read_at(fd, name, limit):
++    item = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=fd)
++    try:
++        st = os.fstat(item)
++        _private(st)
++        if st.st_size > limit:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        chunks, size = [], 0
++        while True:
++            chunk = os.read(item, min(65536, limit + 1 - size))
++            if not chunk:
++                break
++            chunks.append(chunk); size += len(chunk)
++            if size > limit:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++        after = os.fstat(item)
++        if (st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns, st.st_ctime_ns) != (after.st_size, after.st_mtime_ns, after.st_ctime_ns):
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        if _identity(os.stat(name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)) != _identity(st):
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        return b''.join(chunks)
++    finally:
++        os.close(item)
++
++
++def _view(path):
++    cap = current(required=True)
++    parts = _parts(path, cap)
++    tx = _TX.get()
++    if tx is not None:
++        tx.check()
++    return (tx, '/'.join(parts[1:])) if tx is not None and parts[0] == 'skills' else (None, None)
++
++
++def read_bytes(path):
++    tx, rel = _view(path)
++    if tx is not None:
++        if rel not in tx.files:
++            raise FileNotFoundError(str(path))
++        return tx.files[rel]
++    try:
++        with _directory(Path(path).parent) as (fd, check):
++            data = _read_at(fd, Path(path).name, _limit(path))
++            check()
++            return data
++    except FileNotFoundError:
++        raise
++    except OSError:
++        raise ScopeDenied() from None
++
++
++def read_text(path, encoding='utf-8-sig', errors='replace'):
++    return read_bytes(path).decode(encoding, errors)
++
++
++def exists(path):
++    tx, rel = _view(path)
++    if tx is not None:
++        return rel in tx.files or rel in tx.dirs
++    try:
++        with _directory(Path(path).parent) as (fd, check):
++            st = os.stat(Path(path).name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
++            _private(st, stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode))
++            check()
++            return True
++    except FileNotFoundError:
++        return False
++
++
++def validate_directory(path, missing_ok=False):
++    tx, rel = _view(path)
++    if tx is not None:
++        if rel not in tx.dirs and not (missing_ok and rel not in tx.files):
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        return
++    try:
++        with _directory(path):
++            pass
++    except FileNotFoundError:
++        if not missing_ok:
++            raise
++
++
++def _names(fd):
++    # scandir's incremental iterator bounds even a malicious huge directory;
++    # listdir would allocate all names before the entry-limit check.
++    names = []
++    with os.scandir(fd) as entries:
++        for entry in entries:
++            if len(names) >= MAX_ENTRIES:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            names.append(entry.name)
++    return names
++
++
++def _snapshot_fd(fd):
++    """Capture a private native tree from an already owned descriptor.
++
++    Does not grant authority or traverse home paths. Also usable during
++    cleanup after scope revocation, while the original writer fence is held.
++    """
++    files, dirs, identities, total = {}, {''}, {}, 0
++    def visit(fd, prefix, depth):
++        nonlocal total
++        if depth > MAX_DEPTH:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        for name in sorted(_names(fd)):
++            rel = prefix + name
++            if len(files) + len(dirs) >= MAX_ENTRIES:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            st = os.stat(name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
++            isdir = stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)
++            _private(st, isdir)
++            identities[rel] = (_identity(st), st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns, st.st_ctime_ns, st.st_mode)
++            if isdir:
++                sub = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fd)
++                try:
++                    if _identity(os.fstat(sub)) != _identity(st):
++                        raise ScopeDenied()
++                    dirs.add(rel)
++                    visit(sub, rel + '/', depth + 1)
++                finally:
++                    os.close(sub)
++            else:
++                data = _read_at(fd, name, MAX_STATE if name in {'.curator_ledger.jsonl', '.usage.json'} else MAX_FILE)
++                total += len(data)
++                if total > MAX_TREE:
++                    raise ScopeDenied()
++                files[rel] = data
++            after = os.stat(name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
++            if identities[rel] != (_identity(after), after.st_size, after.st_mtime_ns, after.st_ctime_ns, after.st_mode):
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++    _private(os.fstat(fd), True)
++    identity = _identity(os.fstat(fd))
++    visit(fd, '', 0)
++    return files, dirs, (identity, identities)
++
++
++def _snapshot(root):
++    entered = False
++    try:
++        with _directory(root) as (fd, check):
++            entered = True
++            result = _snapshot_fd(fd)
++            check()
++            return result
++    except FileNotFoundError:
++        if entered:
++            raise ScopeDenied() from None
++        return {}, {''}, None
++
++
++def _verify_materialized(parent, name, identity, manifest, files, dirs):
++    """Bind bytes, shape, inode identities and modes to the validated tx.
++
++    This is a consistency check within the admitted writer fence, not an
++    exclusion primitive against arbitrary same-UID host code.
++    """
++    fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent)
++    try:
++        actual_files, actual_dirs, actual = _snapshot_fd(fd)
++        if (actual[0] != identity or actual_files != files or actual_dirs != dirs
++                or set(actual[1]) != set(manifest)
++                or any((info[0], info[-1]) != manifest[rel] for rel, info in actual[1].items())
++                or _identity(os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)) != identity):
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        return actual
++    finally:
++        os.close(fd)
++
++
++def walk_files(root):
++    tx, rel = _view(root)
++    if tx is not None:
++        prefix = rel + '/' if rel else ''
++        result = tuple(Path(root) / p[len(prefix):] for p in sorted(tx.files) if p.startswith(prefix))
++        tx.check()
++        return result
++    files, _, _ = _snapshot(Path(root))
++    current(required=True).check()
++    return tuple(Path(root) / p for p in sorted(files))
++
++
++def mkdir(path, *, exist_ok=True):
++    tx, rel = _view(path)
++    if tx is not None:
++        if rel in tx.files or (not exist_ok and rel in tx.dirs):
++            raise FileExistsError(str(path))
++        parts = rel.split('/') if rel else []
++        for n in range(1, len(parts) + 1):
++            parent = '/'.join(parts[:n])
++            if parent in tx.files:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            tx.dirs.add(parent)
++        return
++    with _directory(path, create=True):
++        pass
++
++
++def write_bytes(path, data):
++    if not isinstance(data, bytes) or len(data) > _limit(path):
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    tx, rel = _view(path)
++    if tx is not None:
++        if rel in tx.dirs:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        mkdir(Path(path).parent)
++        tx.files[rel] = data
++        tx.bounded()
++        return
++    with _directory(Path(path).parent, create=True) as (fd, check):
++        name = Path(path).name
++        try:
++            old = os.stat(name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
++            _private(old)
++        except FileNotFoundError:
++            old = None
++        tmp = '.tmp-' + uuid.uuid4().hex
++        item = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600, dir_fd=fd)
++        try:
++            with os.fdopen(item, 'wb') as stream:
++                stream.write(data); stream.flush(); os.fsync(stream.fileno())
++            check()
++            now = os.stat(name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False) if exists(path) else None
++            if (None if old is None else _identity(old)) != (None if now is None else _identity(now)):
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            os.replace(tmp, name, src_dir_fd=fd, dst_dir_fd=fd)
++            os.fsync(fd)
++            check()
++        finally:
++            try:
++                os.unlink(tmp, dir_fd=fd)
++            except FileNotFoundError:
++                pass
++
++
++def write_text(path, content, **unused):
++    write_bytes(path, content.encode('utf-8'))
++
++
++def remove(path, *, tree=False, missing_ok=False):
++    tx, rel = _view(path)
++    if tx is not None:
++        if not rel:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        if tree:
++            if rel not in tx.dirs:
++                raise FileNotFoundError(str(path))
++            tx.files = {k: v for k, v in tx.files.items() if k != rel and not k.startswith(rel + '/')}
++            tx.dirs = {k for k in tx.dirs if k != rel and not k.startswith(rel + '/')}
++        elif rel in tx.files:
++            del tx.files[rel]
++        elif not missing_ok:
++            raise FileNotFoundError(str(path))
++        return
++    if tree:
++        raise ScopeDenied()  # tree deletes require atomic native transaction
++    with _directory(Path(path).parent) as (fd, check):
++        try:
++            _private(os.stat(Path(path).name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False))
++            check()
++            os.unlink(Path(path).name, dir_fd=fd)
++            check()
++        except FileNotFoundError:
++            if not missing_ok:
++                raise
++
++
++def _erase_at(parent, name, budget=None, depth=0, *, expected=None, manifest=None, prefix=''):
++    """Reclaim captured private storage while every admitted writer is fenced.
++
++    Callers must verify the complete materialized tree first. Path-based unlink
++    is safe only inside this cooperative product ownership boundary; an
++    arbitrary same-UID host can still substitute a name at the syscall. This
++    helper does not claim security against a compromised host. Unknown entries
++    observed before reclamation are retained for explicit reconciliation.
++    """
++    from hermes_cli.skill_lock import require_skill_lock
++    # Retained transaction supplies ownership even after its authority expires.
++    tx = _TX.get()
++    if tx is None or expected is None or manifest is None:
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    require_skill_lock(tx.cap.home, authority=False)
++    budget = [MAX_ENTRIES] if budget is None else budget
++    if depth > MAX_DEPTH:
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent)
++    try:
++        identity = _identity(os.fstat(fd))
++        if expected is not None and identity != expected:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        children = _names(fd)
++        if manifest is not None:
++            wanted = {rel[len(prefix):].split('/')[0] for rel in manifest if rel.startswith(prefix)}
++            if set(children) != wanted:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++        for child in children:
++            if budget[0] <= 0:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            budget[0] -= 1
++            rel = prefix + child
++            st = os.stat(child, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
++            child_identity = _identity(st)
++            if manifest is not None and manifest.get(rel) != child_identity:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            if stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode):
++                _erase_at(fd, child, budget, depth + 1, expected=child_identity,
++                          manifest=manifest, prefix=rel + '/')
++            else:
++                os.unlink(child, dir_fd=fd)
++        if _identity(os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)) != identity:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++    finally:
++        os.close(fd)
++    os.rmdir(name, dir_fd=parent)
++
++
++class _Transaction:
++    def __init__(self):
++        self.cap = current(required=True)
++        require_skill_lock(self.cap.home)
++        # A failed/crashed transaction owns at most one bounded retained tree.
++        # Never adopt unknown remnants or accumulate another stage behind it.
++        with _directory(self.cap.home) as (fd, check):
++            if any(name.startswith('.skills-txn-') for name in _names(fd)):
++                raise RuntimeError('skill_tree_reconciliation_required')
++            check()
++        self.root = self.cap.home / 'skills'
++        self.files, self.dirs, self.original = _snapshot(self.root)
++        self.before = dict(self.files)
++        self.before_dirs = set(self.dirs)
++        self.abort = False
++        self.published = False
++
++    def check(self):
++        if current(required=True) is not self.cap:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        self.cap.check()
++
++    def bounded(self):
++        if len(self.files) + len(self.dirs) > MAX_ENTRIES or sum(map(len, self.files.values())) > MAX_TREE:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++
++    def publish(self):
++        self.check(); self.bounded()
++        require_skill_lock(self.cap.home)
++        if self.abort or (self.files == self.before and self.dirs == self.before_dirs):
++            return
++        # Fail before effects if atomic directory exchange is unavailable.
++        libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
++        rename = getattr(libc, 'renameat2', None)
++        if rename is None:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        rename.argtypes = [ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_uint]
++        rename.restype = ctypes.c_int
++        with _directory(self.cap.home) as (homefd, check):
++            stage = '.skills-txn-' + uuid.uuid4().hex
++            os.mkdir(stage, 0o700, dir_fd=homefd)
++            stage_identity = None
++            stage_manifest = {}
++            publication_conflict = False
++            try:
++                stagefd = os.open(stage, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=homefd)
++                stage_identity = _identity(os.fstat(stagefd))
++                fds = {'': stagefd}
++                try:
++                    if _names(stagefd):
++                        raise ScopeDenied()
++                    for rel in sorted(self.dirs - {''}, key=lambda s: (s.count('/'), s)):
++                        parent, _, leaf = rel.rpartition('/')
++                        os.mkdir(leaf, 0o700, dir_fd=fds[parent])
++                        fds[rel] = os.open(leaf, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=fds[parent])
++                        stage_manifest[rel] = (_identity(os.fstat(fds[rel])), os.fstat(fds[rel]).st_mode)
++                    for rel, data in sorted(self.files.items()):
++                        parent, _, leaf = rel.rpartition('/')
++                        mode = self.original[1].get(rel, (0o600,))[-1] & 0o700 if self.original else 0o600
++                        item = os.open(leaf, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, mode, dir_fd=fds[parent])
++                        stage_manifest[rel] = (_identity(os.fstat(item)), os.fstat(item).st_mode)
++                        with os.fdopen(item, 'wb') as stream:
++                            stream.write(data); stream.flush(); os.fsync(stream.fileno())
++                    for fd in fds.values():
++                        os.fsync(fd)
++                finally:
++                    for fd in reversed(list(fds.values())):
++                        os.close(fd)
++                files, dirs, identity = _snapshot(self.root)
++                if identity != self.original or files != self.before or dirs != self.before_dirs:
++                    raise ScopeDenied()
++                check(); self.check()
++                if _identity(os.stat(stage, dir_fd=homefd, follow_symlinks=False)) != stage_identity:
++                    publication_conflict = True
++                    raise RuntimeError('skill_tree_staging_identity_conflict')
++                try:
++                    _verify_materialized(homefd, stage, stage_identity, stage_manifest, self.files, self.dirs)
++                except (OSError, ScopeDenied):
++                    publication_conflict = True
++                    raise RuntimeError('skill_tree_staging_content_conflict') from None
++                check(); self.check()
++                if rename(homefd, stage.encode(), homefd, b'skills', 2 if self.original else 1):
++                    raise ScopeDenied()
++                self.published = True
++                if (_identity(os.stat('skills', dir_fd=homefd, follow_symlinks=False)) != stage_identity
++                        or (self.original and _identity(os.stat(stage, dir_fd=homefd, follow_symlinks=False)) != self.original[0])):
++                    publication_conflict = True
++                    raise RuntimeError('skill_tree_publication_identity_conflict')
++                try:
++                    _verify_materialized(homefd, 'skills', stage_identity, stage_manifest, self.files, self.dirs)
++                except (OSError, ScopeDenied):
++                    publication_conflict = True
++                    raise RuntimeError('skill_tree_published_content_conflict') from None
++                os.fsync(homefd)
++                check(); self.check()
++            finally:
++                # Cleanup owns only a verified materialized tree under this
++                # same fence. Any unknown artifact is retained, and the next
++                # transaction refuses before allocating further storage.
++                try:
++                    if (not publication_conflict and stage_identity is not None
++                            and (not self.published or self.original is not None)):
++                        expected = self.original[0] if self.published and self.original else stage_identity
++                        manifest = ({rel: (info[0], info[-1]) for rel, info in self.original[1].items()}
++                                    if self.published and self.original else stage_manifest)
++                        expected_files = self.before if self.published and self.original else self.files
++                        expected_dirs = self.before_dirs if self.published and self.original else self.dirs
++                        # On failed partial construction this check retains the
++                        # bounded remnant rather than adopting partial contents.
++                        _verify_materialized(homefd, stage, expected, manifest, expected_files, expected_dirs)
++                        _erase_at(homefd, stage, expected=expected,
++                                  manifest={rel: info[0] for rel, info in manifest.items()})
++
++                except (OSError, ScopeDenied):
++                    if self.published:
++                        raise RuntimeError("skill_tree_published_cleanup_incomplete") from None
++                    raise RuntimeError("skill_tree_not_published_cleanup_incomplete") from None
++
++
++@contextmanager
++def transaction():
++    existing = _TX.get()
++    if existing is not None:
++        existing.check()
++        yield existing
++        return
++    from tools.skill_usage import skill_file_lock
++    cap = current(required=True)
++    with skill_file_lock(cap.home / 'skills' / '.locks' / 'transaction.lock'):
++        tx = _Transaction()
++        token = _TX.set(tx)
++        try:
++            yield tx
++            tx.publish()
++        finally:
++            _TX.reset(token)

++class DocumentPath:
++    """Minimal data-only path protocol for the native static scanner/linter.
++
++    No __fspath__: consumers cannot silently turn this capability into an
++    unrestricted filesystem Path. All reads and enumeration stay in this seam.
++    """
++    def __init__(self, path):
++        self.path = Path(path)
++        _parts(self.path, current(required=True))
++
++    def __str__(self):
++        return str(self.path)
++
++    def __truediv__(self, part):
++        return DocumentPath(self.path / part)
++
++    @property
++    def name(self):
++        return self.path.name
++
++    @property
++    def suffix(self):
++        return self.path.suffix
++
++    @property
++    def parts(self):
++        return self.path.parts
++
++    def relative_to(self, other):
++        return self.path.relative_to(other.path if isinstance(other, DocumentPath) else other)
++
++    def exists(self):
++        return exists(self.path)
++
++    def is_dir(self):
++        tx, rel = _view(self.path)
++        if tx is not None:
++            return rel in tx.dirs
++        if not exists(self.path):
++            return False
++        with _directory(self.path.parent) as (fd, check):
++            st = os.stat(self.name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)
++            _private(st, stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode))
++            check()
++            return stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode)
++
++    def is_file(self):
++        return self.exists() and not self.is_dir()
++
++    def is_symlink(self):
++        self.exists()  # refuses any redirect, including inward ones
++        return False
++
++    def read_text(self, **kwargs):
++        return read_text(self.path, **kwargs)
++
++    def read_bytes(self):
++        return read_bytes(self.path)
++
++    def stat(self):
++        from types import SimpleNamespace
++        tx, rel = _view(self.path)
++        if tx is None or rel not in tx.files:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        mode = tx.original[1].get(rel, (0o600,))[-1] if tx.original else 0o600
++        return SimpleNamespace(st_size=len(tx.files[rel]), st_mode=stat.S_IFREG | (mode & 0o700))
++
++    def rglob(self, pattern):
++        import fnmatch
++        return tuple(DocumentPath(p) for p in walk_files(self.path)
++                     if pattern == '*' or fnmatch.fnmatch(p.name, pattern))

++"""One native publication fence for marked user homes, including admin writers.
++
++The marker selects a lock, never grants a UserScope or ordinary authorization.
++All managed locks collapse to <home>/.skills.lock; no inner per-skill/ledger lock
++may precede it. Reentrancy is per thread and retains the original descriptor.
++Unmarked native homes keep their existing locking and platform behavior.
++"""
++from contextlib import contextmanager
++from functools import wraps
++import os
++from pathlib import Path
++import stat
++import threading
++
++from hermes_cli.friday_user_scope import current, MARKER, ScopeDenied
++
++_HELD = threading.local()
++
++
++def _identity(st):
++    return st.st_dev, st.st_ino
++
++
++def _private(st, directory=False):
++    if (st.st_uid != os.getuid() or st.st_mode & 0o077
++            or (directory and not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode))
++            or (not directory and (not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_nlink != 1))):
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++
++
++def _held():
++    if not hasattr(_HELD, 'homes'):
++        _HELD.homes = {}
++    return _HELD.homes
++
++
++def require_skill_lock(home, *, authority=True):
++    """Cleanup may only use storage owned by the current publication window."""
++    entry = _held().get(Path(home))
++    if entry is None or entry[0] != os.getpid():
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    entry[1](authority=authority)
++
++
++@contextmanager
++def managed_home_lock(home):
++    """Yield True under the managed fence, False for an unmarked donor home.
++
++    Home/marker/lock descriptors and their ancestor links are checked before
++    and after flock's actual wait. A replaced lock is refused, never reopened
++    to silently follow a different inode. No path inside skills/ is a lock.
++    """
++    home = Path(home)
++    cap = current()
++    if cap is not None and home != cap.home:
++        raise ScopeDenied()
++    held = _held()
++    entry = held.get(home)
++    if entry is not None:
++        if entry[0] != os.getpid():
++            raise ScopeDenied()  # fork must not inherit a false reentrant claim
++        entry[1]()
++        if cap is not None:
++            cap.check()
++        yield True
++        entry[1]()
++        if cap is not None:
++            cap.check()
++        return
++    try:
++        os.lstat(home / MARKER)
++    except FileNotFoundError:
++        if cap is not None:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        yield False
++        return
++    if (not home.is_absolute() or '..' in home.parts or held):
++        raise ScopeDenied()  # one managed home at a time; no cross-home lock cycle
++    try:
++        import fcntl
++    except ImportError:
++        raise ScopeDenied() from None
++    fds, links = [], []
++    item = None
++    locked = False
++    try:
++        fds.append(os.open('/', os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY))
++        for name in home.parts[1:]:
++            parent = fds[-1]
++            fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW, dir_fd=parent)
++            fds.append(fd)
++            links.append((parent, name, _identity(os.fstat(fd))))
++        homefd = fds[-1]
++        _private(os.fstat(homefd), True)
++        if cap is not None and _identity(os.fstat(homefd)) != cap.directory_identity:
++            raise ScopeDenied()
++        marker = os.open(MARKER, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=homefd)
++        fds.append(marker)
++        _private(os.fstat(marker))
++        marker_identity = _identity(os.fstat(marker))
++        item = os.open('.skills.lock', os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK,
++                       0o600, dir_fd=homefd)
++        _private(os.fstat(item))
++        lock_identity = _identity(os.fstat(item))
++
++        def check(*, authority=True):
++            if authority and cap is not None:
++                cap.check()
++            for parent, name, identity in links:
++                st = os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
++                if not stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) or _identity(st) != identity:
++                    raise ScopeDenied()
++            _private(os.fstat(homefd), True)
++            for name, fd, identity in ((MARKER, marker, marker_identity), ('.skills.lock', item, lock_identity)):
++                st = os.stat(name, dir_fd=homefd, follow_symlinks=False)
++                _private(st); _private(os.fstat(fd))
++                if _identity(st) != identity:
++                    raise ScopeDenied()
++
++        check()
++        fcntl.flock(item, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
++        locked = True
++        check()
++        held[home] = (os.getpid(), check)
++        yield True
++        check()
++    finally:
++        held.pop(home, None)
++        if item is not None:
++            try:
++                if locked:
++                    fcntl.flock(item, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
++            finally:
++                os.close(item)
++        for fd in reversed(fds):
++            os.close(fd)
++
++
++def skill_write_window(home, *, admin_only=False, with_arguments=False):
++    """Fence an existing native operation, including its validation and metadata.
++
++    `home` is a call-time resolver for the actual destination, not a cached
++    profile. The body/feature gates and unmarked donor semantics are retained.
++    """
++    def decorate(fn):
++        @wraps(fn)
++        def fenced(*args, **kwargs):
++            if admin_only and current() is not None:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            with managed_home_lock(home(*args, **kwargs) if with_arguments else home()):
++                return fn(*args, **kwargs)
++        return fenced
++    return decorate
++
++
++def managed_output(path):
++    """An output of this already-fenced native operation, never guessed authority."""
++    path = Path(path)
++    for home in _held():
++        if path.is_relative_to(home):
++            require_skill_lock(home)
++            if '..' in path.parts:
++                raise ScopeDenied()
++            return home
++    return None
++
++
++def native_mkdir(path, mode=0o777, parents=False, exist_ok=False):
++    path = Path(path)
++    home = managed_output(path)
++    if home is None:
++        return path.mkdir(mode=mode, parents=parents, exist_ok=exist_ok)
++    # Path.mkdir(parents=True, mode=0700) leaves intermediate parents at0777.
++    # Create each explicit output directory privately; never chmod old objects.
++    if path == home:
++        _private(path.lstat(), True)
++        if not exist_ok:
++            raise FileExistsError(str(path))
++        return
++    if parents:
++        native_mkdir(path.parent, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
++    else:
++        _private(path.parent.lstat(), True)
++    try:
++        path.mkdir(mode=0o700)
++    except FileExistsError:
++        if not exist_ok:
++            raise
++        _private(path.lstat(), True)
++
++
++def native_open(path, mode='r', **kwargs):
++    """Explicit private creation for managed native outputs; old bytes untouched
++    on incompatible mode/type/link. Donor open semantics remain unchanged."""
++    import builtins
++    path = Path(path)
++    home = managed_output(path)
++    if home is None or not any(c in mode for c in 'wax+'):
++        return builtins.open(path, mode, **kwargs)
++    native_mkdir(path.parent, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
++    def opener(name, flags):
++        fd = os.open(name, (flags & ~os.O_TRUNC) | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, 0o600)
++        try:
++            _private(os.fstat(fd))
++            if flags & os.O_TRUNC:
++                os.ftruncate(fd, 0)
++            return fd
++        except BaseException:
++            os.close(fd)
++            raise
++    return builtins.open(path, mode, opener=opener, **kwargs)
++
++
++def native_write_text(path, data, encoding=None, errors=None, newline=None):
++    with native_open(path, 'w', encoding=encoding, errors=errors, newline=newline) as stream:
++        return stream.write(data)
++
++
++def native_write_bytes(path, data):
++    with native_open(path, 'wb') as stream:
++        return stream.write(data)
++
++
++def _atomic_mode(path, kwargs):
++    path = Path(path)
++    if managed_output(path) is not None:
++        native_mkdir(path.parent, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
++        try:
++            old = path.lstat()
++            _private(old)
++            mode = old.st_mode & 0o700
++        except FileNotFoundError:
++            mode = 0o600
++        kwargs['mode'] = mode
++    return kwargs
++
++
++def atomic_write_text(path, content, **kwargs):
++    from utils import atomic_write_text as native
++    return native(path, content, **_atomic_mode(path, kwargs))
++
++
++def atomic_json_write(path, data, **kwargs):
++    from utils import atomic_json_write as native
++    return native(path, data, **_atomic_mode(path, kwargs))
++
++
++def mkdir_under_hermes_home(path):
++    from hermes_constants import assert_named_profile_home_live
++    assert_named_profile_home_live(path)
++    native_mkdir(path, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
++    return Path(path)
++
++
++def native_copy2(src, dest, **kwargs):
++    import shutil
++    dest = Path(dest)
++    if managed_output(dest) is None:
++        return shutil.copy2(src, dest, **kwargs)
++    # Only a freshly-created copy is normalized; never chmod an old owner file.
++    if dest.exists() or dest.is_symlink():
++        raise FileExistsError(str(dest))
++    native_mkdir(dest.parent, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
++    with open(src, 'rb') as source, native_open(dest, 'xb') as output:
++        shutil.copyfileobj(source, output)
++        st = os.stat(src)
++        os.fchmod(output.fileno(), st.st_mode & 0o700)
++        os.utime(output.fileno(), ns=(st.st_atime_ns, st.st_mtime_ns))
++    return str(dest)
++
++
++def native_copytree(src, dest, *, ignore=None):
++    import shutil
++    dest = Path(dest)
++    if managed_output(dest) is None:
++        return shutil.copytree(src, dest, ignore=ignore)
++    # Build actual outputs privately instead of copytree's final copystat,
++    # which would reintroduce donor0644/0755. No old object is chmodded/adopted.
++    native_mkdir(dest, parents=True, exist_ok=False)
++    names = os.listdir(src)
++    ignored = set(ignore(str(src), names) if ignore else ())
++    for name in names:
++        if name in ignored:
++            continue
++        source = Path(src) / name
++        if source.is_dir():
++            native_copytree(source, dest / name, ignore=ignore)
++        else:
++            native_copy2(source, dest / name)
++    st = os.stat(src)
++    os.utime(dest, ns=(st.st_atime_ns, st.st_mtime_ns))
++    return str(dest)

+def native_browser_check(config, *, rendered_only=False):
+    """Mandatory native registration/readiness; never substitute tool omission.
+
+    This is source/install readiness only. It grants neither ordinary SAFE
+    capabilities nor the separate native loopback/egress execution admission.
+    """
+    from scripts.friday_install import require
+    from hermes_cli.browser_runtime import local_readiness, REQUIRED_BROWSER_TOOLS, friday_browser_config
+    require(config.get('browser') == friday_browser_config(), 'mandatory_browser_config_missing')
+    local_readiness(config)
+    if not rendered_only:
+        from hermes_cli.config import read_raw_config
+        require(read_raw_config().get('browser') == config['browser'], 'native_browser_profile_mismatch')
+        import tools.browser_tool  # Native registrations, no browser command.
+        from tools.registry import registry
+        for name in REQUIRED_BROWSER_TOOLS:
+            entry = registry.get_entry(name)
+            require(entry is not None and entry.toolset == 'browser'
+                    and entry.handler.__module__ == 'tools.browser_tool'
+                    and callable(entry.check_fn) and entry.check_fn() is True,
+                    'mandatory_native_browser_registry_unavailable')

+"""Native UserScope/session consumers; sources only until repaired guard admission.
+
+Real onboarding/admission generation, native WebCall/BrowserOwner/ProcessRegistry
+and private profile files; synthetic agent inputs and client handle, no
+CLI/browser/network/model process. Independent live proof is required.
+"""
+from contextlib import contextmanager
+import json
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+from types import SimpleNamespace

+from hermes_cli import friday_user_scope as scope
+from hermes_cli import browser_runtime as runtime

+def test_normal_profile_fixes_browser_driver_without_expanding_ordinary_authority():
+    from tools.configure_product import compose_product, USER_TOOLSETS
+    bundle = compose_product(inputs())
+    assert bundle['config']['browser'] == runtime.friday_browser_config()
+    assert template(bundle)['config']['browser'] == bundle['config']['browser']
+    assert 'skills' in USER_TOOLSETS
+    assert {'skills_list', 'skill_view', 'skill_manage'} <= scope.SAFE
+    assert not runtime.REQUIRED_BROWSER_TOOLS & scope.SAFE
+    assert 'browser' not in USER_TOOLSETS  # Pending boundary, not accepted exclusion.
+    assert bundle['config']['fallback_providers'] == []
+    assert bundle['config']['delegation']['fallback_providers'] == []

+@pytest.fixture
+def browser_users(env, monkeypatch):
+    # Inject ONLY the already known missing page/CDP network dependency. The
+    # real admission runs first, including actual owner and unresolved custody
+    # checks; no other refusal is converted into a unit-fixture allowance.
+    from tools import process_registry as processes
+    registry = processes.ProcessRegistry()
+    monkeypatch.setattr(processes, 'process_registry', registry)
+    env.browser_test_registry = registry
+    env.browser_test_real_admission = registry.require_browser_execution
+    def missing_network_only(owner):
+        try:
+            env.browser_test_real_admission(owner)
+        except RuntimeError as exc:
+            if str(exc) != ('browser_execution_boundary_unavailable: '
+                            'REQUIRED_UNRESOLVED_NATIVE_PAGE_EGRESS_AND_OWNED_CDP_STREAM'):
+                raise
+        else:
+            pytest.fail('unit fixture requires the explicitly unresolved network boundary')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(registry, 'require_browser_execution', missing_network_only)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'require_sandbox_host', lambda: None)
+    from tools import interrupt
+    monkeypatch.setattr(interrupt, 'is_interrupted', lambda: False)
+    env.browser_test_deadline = time.monotonic() + 60
+    env.browser_test_calls = []
+    clock_token = runtime._BROWSER_DEADLINE.set(env.browser_test_deadline)
+    try:
+        env.template['config']['browser'] = runtime.friday_browser_config()
+        save(env, env.cfg)
+        complete(env, '1'); complete(env, '2')
+        from tools import browser_tool as bt
+        from tools import browser_tool_session as session
+        from tools import browser_tool_lifecycle as lifecycle
+        for name in ('_active_sessions', '_last_active_session_key', '_session_last_activity',
+                     '_session_owner_homes', '_cleanup_failures', '_suspect_browser_sessions'):
+            monkeypatch.setattr(bt, name, {})
+        monkeypatch.setattr(bt, '_recording_sessions', set())
+        monkeypatch.setattr(lifecycle, '_start_browser_cleanup_thread', lambda: None)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(session._cdp, '_ensure_cdp_supervisor', lambda *a: pytest.fail('managed legacy supervisor lookup'))
+        monkeypatch.setattr(session._cdp, '_stop_cdp_supervisor', lambda *a: None)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(bt, '_socket_safe_tmpdir', lambda: str(env.home / 'test-sockets'))
+        env.browser_test_real_popen = session._popen_agent_browser
+        monkeypatch.setattr(session, '_popen_agent_browser', lambda *a, **kw: pytest.fail('no CLI in source unit controls'))
+        yield env, bt, session, lifecycle
+    finally:
+        try:
+            for call in reversed(env.browser_test_calls):
+                owner = getattr(call, 'browser_owner', None)
+                with scope.authority(owner.scope.home if owner is not None else env.home):
+                    receipt = call.turn.stop()
+                if call.session is None:
+                    # Only native call membership was empty; this is not a
+                    # claim that a retained browser dictionary was reaped.
+                    assert receipt['status'] == 'QUIESCENT' and not call.turn.calls
+                else:
+                    assert receipt['status'] == 'STOP_UNCONFIRMED'
+                    assert call.session.web_stop_requested and not call.session.web_settled
+                    assert registry._running[call.session.id] is call.session
+        finally:
+            runtime._BROWSER_DEADLINE.reset(clock_token)
+

+@contextmanager
+def native_browser_call(env, task_id, uid='1'):
+    """Use the same producer/binder as tool_executor, with source-unit inputs.
+
+    No WebCall/BrowserOwner is synthesized or patched. The one fixture ceiling
+    is retained for every call; actual bind exit controls turn membership.
+    """
+    from agent.web_call_context import make_web_call, bind_web_call, current_web_call
+    from tools.approval_context import set_current_session_key, reset_current_session_key
+    agent = SimpleNamespace(session_id='browser-fixture-session-' + uid,
+                            _current_turn_id='browser-fixture-turn-' + uid,
+                            _web_run_deadline=env.browser_test_deadline)
+    token = set_current_session_key('browser-fixture-routing-' + uid)
+    try:
+        call = make_web_call(agent, env.browser_test_deadline, task_id,
+                             'browser-fixture-call-' + str(len(env.browser_test_calls)))
+        env.browser_test_calls.append(call)
+        with bind_web_call(call):
+            assert current_web_call() is call and call in call.turn.calls
+            assert call.deadline() == env.browser_test_deadline
+            yield call
+        if call.session is None:
+            assert call not in call.turn.calls
+        else:
+            assert call in call.turn.calls  # Unresolved custody stays attached.
+    finally:
+        reset_current_session_key(token)
+
+
+def enter_source(env, uid):
+    ok, source = admitted(env, uid)
+    assert ok
+    return source
+

+def test_same_native_task_is_separate_for_two_actual_principals(browser_users):
+    env, bt, session, lifecycle = browser_users
+    one, two = enter_source(env, '1'), enter_source(env, '2')
+    # Keep the original native binding alive while the other principal calls;
+    # exiting bind_web_call would correctly discard a call with no worker.
+    with native_browser_call(env, 'same-task') as first:
+        with scope.authority(home(env, '1')), scope.scoped_source(one):
+            key1 = scope.browser_task_key('same-task')
+            no_clock = runtime._BROWSER_DEADLINE.set(None)
+            try:
+                with pytest.raises(runtime.BrowserPolicyError, match='browser_original_deadline_required'):
+                    session._get_session_info(key1)
+                assert key1 not in bt._active_sessions and key1 not in bt._session_last_activity
+            finally:
+                runtime._BROWSER_DEADLINE.reset(no_clock)
+            with pytest.raises(runtime.BrowserPolicyError, match='browser_original_deadline_exhausted'):
+                session._get_session_info(key1, deadline=0.0)
+            assert key1 not in bt._active_sessions and key1 not in bt._session_last_activity
+            # browser_navigate uses this default-None argument under an already
+            # bound command clock. Do not hide its contract with an explicit arg.
+            info1 = session._get_session_info(key1)
+            retained = info1['_friday_execution_owner']
+            assert retained.call is first and retained.scope is scope.current(required=True)
+            assert retained.original_deadline == env.browser_test_deadline
+            bt._last_active_session_key[key1] = key1
+            assert bt._last_session_key('same-task') == key1
+        with scope.authority(home(env, '2')), scope.scoped_source(two), native_browser_call(env, 'same-task', '2') as second:
+            key2 = scope.browser_task_key('same-task')
+            info2 = session._get_session_info(key2)
+            assert info2['_friday_execution_owner'].call is second
+            assert first.turn is not second.turn and first in first.turn.calls
+            assert key1 != key2 and info1['session_name'] != info2['session_name']
+            with pytest.raises(scope.ScopeDenied):
+                session._get_session_info(key1)
+            assert bt._active_sessions[key1] is info1  # Foreign caller cannot revoke valid owner.
+            assert not info1.get('_friday_stop_unconfirmed')
+            with pytest.raises(PermissionError, match='navigation_required'):
+                bt._last_session_key(key1)  # Raw input resembling another key is re-namespaced.
+        with scope.authority(home(env, '1')), scope.scoped_source(one):
+            assert retained.check() == env.browser_test_deadline
+            assert bt._active_sessions[key1] is info1
+

+def test_revocation_reaps_retained_session_and_cannot_recreate(browser_users):
+    env, bt, session, lifecycle = browser_users
+    owner = enter_source(env, '1')
+    with scope.authority(home(env)), scope.scoped_source(owner), native_browser_call(env, 'task'):
+        key = scope.browser_task_key('task')
+        info = session._get_session_info(key)
+    env.admin.set_user('default', **ident(), enabled=False, role='user')
+    # Janitor has no current principal: it validates the retained capability in
+    # its recorded owner's home, then uses existing cleanup without a new session.
+    lifecycle._cleanup_inactive_browser_sessions()
+    assert bt._active_sessions[key] is info
+    assert info['_friday_stop_unconfirmed']
+    assert info['_friday_user_scope'].revoked
+    assert activate(env)['enabled']
+    with scope.authority(home(env)), pytest.raises(scope.ScopeDenied):
+        with scope.scoped_source(owner):
+            session._get_session_info(key)
+    fresh = enter_source(env, '1')
+    with scope.authority(home(env)), scope.scoped_source(fresh):
+        assert scope.browser_task_key('task') != key
+

+def test_directory_replacement_revokes_retained_browser(browser_users):
+    env, bt, session, lifecycle = browser_users
+    owner = enter_source(env, '1')
+    with scope.authority(home(env)), scope.scoped_source(owner), native_browser_call(env, 'task'):
+        key = scope.browser_task_key('task')
+        info = session._get_session_info(key)
+    folder = home(env)
+    folder.rename(folder.with_name('old-private-home'))
+    folder.mkdir(mode=0o700)
+    assert scope.browser_revoked(info)
+    lifecycle._cleanup_inactive_browser_sessions()
+    assert bt._active_sessions[key] is info
+    assert info['_friday_stop_unconfirmed']

+def test_non_navigation_after_cleanup_does_not_allocate(browser_users):

+def test_unconfirmed_stop_keeps_custody_and_refuses_new_commands(browser_users, monkeypatch):

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('second_state', ['revoked', 'idle'])
+def test_janitor_continues_actual_second_principal_after_first_unconfirmed(browser_users, monkeypatch, second_state):
+    env, bt, session, lifecycle = browser_users
+    records = []
+    for uid in ('1', '2'):
+        source = enter_source(env, uid)
+        with scope.authority(home(env, uid)), scope.scoped_source(source), native_browser_call(env, 'same-task', uid):
+            key = scope.browser_task_key('same-task')
+            records.append((key, session._get_session_info(key)))
+    first, second = records
+    env.admin.set_user('default', **ident('1'), enabled=False, role='user')
+    if second_state == 'revoked':
+        env.admin.set_user('default', **ident('2'), enabled=False, role='user')
+    else:
+        bt._session_last_activity[second[0]] = 0
+    attempted = []
+    release = lifecycle._release_session_resources
+    def observed(key, info, **kwargs):
+        attempted.append(key)
+        return release(key, info, **kwargs)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lifecycle, '_release_session_resources', observed)
+    lifecycle._cleanup_inactive_browser_sessions()
+    assert attempted == [first[0], second[0]]
+    for key, info in records:
+        assert bt._active_sessions[key] is info and info['_friday_stop_unconfirmed']
+        assert bt._cleanup_failures[key] == 1
+        assert key in bt._session_owner_homes and key in bt._session_last_activity

+def test_actual_principal_revocation_before_pid_publication_stops_original_client(browser_users, monkeypatch):
+    env, bt, session, lifecycle = browser_users
+    owner = enter_source(env, '1')
+    events = []
+    with scope.authority(home(env)), scope.scoped_source(owner), native_browser_call(env, 'task'):
+        key = scope.browser_task_key('task')
+        info = session._get_session_info(key)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, 'local_readiness', lambda: {'cli': '/owned/cli', 'executable': '/owned/chrome'})
+        monkeypatch.setattr(session, '_browser_in_sandbox', lambda: False)
+        class Client:
+            pid = 987654321
+            returncode = None
+            def wait(self, timeout=None):
+                events.append('wait')
+                if 'kill' not in events:
+                    env.admin.set_user('default', **ident(), enabled=False, role='user')
+                    raise session.subprocess.TimeoutExpired('original-client', timeout)
+                self.returncode = 0
+                return self.returncode
+            def kill(self):
+                events.append('kill')
+        def spawn(*a, **kw):
+            events.append('spawn')
+            return Client()
+        monkeypatch.setattr(session, '_popen_agent_browser', env.browser_test_real_popen)
+        monkeypatch.setattr(session.subprocess, 'Popen', spawn)
+        def synthetic_client_birth(pid):
+            # This Client is a Python handle double, never a host PID. Keep its
+            # birth unknown instead of reading/adopting an unrelated process.
+            assert pid == Client.pid
+            return None
+        monkeypatch.setattr(env.browser_test_registry, '_safe_host_start_time', synthetic_client_birth)
+        _, result = session._dispatch_browser_command(key, info, '/owned/cli', 'snapshot', [], 10, None,
+                                                      deadline=runtime._BROWSER_DEADLINE.get())
+        assert result['success'] is False and result['custody'] == 'STOP_UNCONFIRMED'
+        assert result['error'] == str(scope.ScopeDenied())
+        assert 'retained_identity_required' in result['stop_error']
+        assert events == ['spawn', 'wait', 'kill', 'wait']
+        assert bt._active_sessions[key] is info
+        record = info['_friday_registry_session']
+        native_owner = info['_friday_execution_owner']
+        assert native_owner.call.session is record
+        assert native_owner.call.registry is env.browser_test_registry
+        assert env.browser_test_registry._running[record.id] is record
+        assert record.process.pid == Client.pid and record.host_start_time is None
+        assert record.web_stop_requested and not record.web_settled
+        assert record.browser_client_wait_confirmed
+        assert record.web_deadline == native_owner.original_deadline == env.browser_test_deadline
+        with pytest.raises(scope.ScopeDenied):
+            session._get_session_info(key)
+        assert events == ['spawn', 'wait', 'kill', 'wait']
+

+"""Native skill dispatch/projections under real two-user onboarding; offline only."""
+from contextlib import contextmanager

+@contextmanager
+def entered(env, uid='1'):
+    ok, source = admitted(env, uid)
+    assert ok
+    token = set_hermes_home_override(str(home(env, uid)))
+    try:
+        with scope.scoped_source(source) as cap:
+            yield cap
+    finally:
+        reset_hermes_home_override(token)
+
+
+def dispatch(tool, **args):
+    result = registry.dispatch(tool, args, task_id='same-task')
+    return json.loads(result) if isinstance(result, str) else result
+
+
+def document(name='procedure', description='Own verified procedure', body='Use authoritative sources.'):
+    return f'---\nname: {name}\ndescription: {description}\n---\n{body}\n'
+
+
+def make(env, uid='1', name='procedure', **kwargs):
+    complete(env, uid)
+    with entered(env, uid):
+        result = dispatch('skill_manage', action='create', name=name, content=document(name, **kwargs))
+        assert result.get('success'), result
+

+        assert '/procedure' in skill_commands.get_skill_commands()
+        assert dispatch('skill_manage', action='patch',name='procedure',old_string='Own verified procedure',new_string='Revised own procedure')['success']
+        assert 'Revised own procedure' in prompt_builder.build_skills_system_prompt()
+        text,names,missing=skill_commands.build_preloaded_skills_prompt(['procedure'])

+def test_two_same_named_skills_do_not_merge_histories_names_or_content(env):

+        with entered(env,uid):

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind',['symlink_file','symlink_dir','hardlink','fifo','oversize'])
+def test_unsafe_document_objects_fail_closed_without_content(env,kind):
+    make(env)
+    p=home(env)/'skills/procedure/SKILL.md'
+    target=env.home/'FOREIGN';target.write_text('FOREIGN_SECRET_SENTINEL');target.chmod(0o600)
+    if kind=='symlink_dir':
+        d=p.parent;d.rename(d.with_name('saved'));d.symlink_to(env.home,target_is_directory=True)
+    else:
+        p.unlink()
+        if kind=='symlink_file':p.symlink_to(target)
+        elif kind=='hardlink':os.link(target,p)
+        elif kind=='fifo':os.mkfifo(p,0o600)
+        elif kind=='oversize':p.write_bytes(b'x'*(io.MAX_FILE+1));p.chmod(0o600)
+    with entered(env):
+        result=dispatch('skill_view',name='procedure')
+        assert not result.get('success') and 'FOREIGN_SECRET_SENTINEL' not in json.dumps(result)
+    assert target.read_text()=='FOREIGN_SECRET_SENTINEL'

+"""Effective native stored-prompt restore across actual product re-admission."""
+import json
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock
+import pytest
+from test_user_onboarding import env,complete,home,ident,save
+from test_ordinary_skills import entered,document
+from test_skill_privacy import install_document,revoke
+from hermes_cli import friday_user_scope as scope
+from hermes_cli import friday_prompt_scope as prompt_scope
+from hermes_state import SessionDB
+from agent import conversation_loop,system_prompt
+
+
+def new_agent(db, caching=False):
+    agent=MagicMock()
+    agent._cached_system_prompt=None
+    agent._cached_system_prompt_static=None
+    agent.session_id='actual-native-own-session'
+    agent.model='local-test';agent.provider='custom';agent.platform='cli'
+    agent._session_db=db;agent._use_prompt_caching=caching
+    agent.enabled_toolsets=agent.disabled_toolsets=None
+    agent._auto_load_skills_resolved=False
+    agent._persist_disabled=False
+    agent.skip_context_files=False
+    agent.valid_tool_names=['skill_view']
+    agent.tools=[]
+    agent._platform_hint_overrides=None
+    agent._session_title_hint=''
+    agent._surface_switch_note='';agent._gateway_turn_context_notes=''
+    agent._build_system_prompt=MagicMock(side_effect=lambda _msg: 'NATIVE PROMPT\n'+''.join(system_prompt._auto_load_parts(agent)))
+    scope.capture_agent(agent)
+    return agent
+
+
+def own_db(env):
+    db=SessionDB(home(env)/'state.db')
+    db.create_session('actual-native-own-session','telegram',user_id='1',profile_name='user-1',
+        origin_json=json.dumps({'friday_account_origin':{'schema':'friday.account_origin.v1',
+            'platform':'telegram','transport_profile':'default','account_id':'bot-A'}}),
+        model_config={'keep_native_field':'PRESERVE'})
+    db.append_message('actual-native-own-session','user','Continue my original task')
+    return db
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('caching',[False,True])
+def test_real_session_reuses_same_admission_but_rebuilds_after_readmission(env,caching):
+    env.template['config']['skills'].update({'auto_load':['procedure']})
+    save(env,env.cfg)
+    complete(env);p=install_document(home(env),document(body='OLD_AUTOLOAD_INSTRUCTIONS'))
+    db=own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env) as first_cap:
+            first=new_agent(db,caching)
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(first,None,[])
+            old=first._cached_system_prompt
+            assert 'OLD_AUTOLOAD_INSTRUCTIONS' in old
+            saved=db.get_session(first.session_id)
+            stored=json.loads(saved['model_config'])
+            assert stored['keep_native_field']=='PRESERVE'
+            assert stored[prompt_scope.FIELD]['generation']==first_cap.admission_generation
+            second=new_agent(db,caching)
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(second,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+            assert second._cached_system_prompt==old
+            second._build_system_prompt.assert_not_called()
+            revoke(env)
+            with pytest.raises(scope.ScopeDenied):conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(second,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+        # Authorized operator reenables the same real principal; this is a new
+        # native admission, not resetting the retained revoked capability.
+        env.admin.set_user('default',**ident(),enabled=True,role='user')
+        p.write_text(document(body='NEW_AUTOLOAD_INSTRUCTIONS'));p.chmod(0o600)
+        with entered(env) as fresh_cap:
+            assert fresh_cap.admission_generation!=first_cap.admission_generation
+            third=new_agent(db,caching)
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(third,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+            effective=third._cached_system_prompt
+            assert 'NEW_AUTOLOAD_INSTRUCTIONS' in effective and 'OLD_AUTOLOAD_INSTRUCTIONS' not in effective
+            third._build_system_prompt.assert_called_once()
+            saved=db.get_session(third.session_id)
+            assert saved['system_prompt']==effective
+            assert json.loads(saved['model_config'])[prompt_scope.FIELD]['generation']==fresh_cap.admission_generation
+            assert db.get_messages(third.session_id)[0]['content']=='Continue my original task'
+    finally:db.close()
+
+
+def test_native_prompt_and_provenance_write_rolls_back_as_one_transaction(env):
+    complete(env);db=own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env):
+            db.update_system_prompt('actual-native-own-session','FIRST')
+            before=db.get_session('actual-native-own-session')
+            # Native preexisting malformed metadata must not be overwritten or
+            # allow a prompt write without matching ownership provenance.
+            db.update_session_meta('actual-native-own-session','malformed: [')
+            with pytest.raises(Exception):db.update_system_prompt('actual-native-own-session','MUST_NOT_PERSIST')
+            after=db.get_session('actual-native-own-session')
+            assert after['system_prompt']==before['system_prompt']=='FIRST'
+            assert after['model_config']=='malformed: ['
+    finally:db.close()
+

+def tool_definition(description):
+    return {'type':'function','function':{'name':'skill_view','description':description,
+            'parameters':{'type':'object','properties':{}}}}
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('missing',[None,''])
+def test_readmission_missing_prompt_never_restores_prior_tool_definitions(env,missing):
+    complete(env);db=own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env):
+            old=new_agent(db);old.tools=[tool_definition('OLD_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(old,None,[])
+            assert 'OLD_DEFINITION' in db.get_session(old.session_id)['tool_names']
+            db.update_system_prompt(old.session_id,missing)
+            revoke(env)
+        env.admin.set_user('default',**ident(),enabled=True,role='user')
+        with entered(env):
+            fresh=new_agent(db);fresh.tools=[tool_definition('NEW_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(fresh,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+            assert 'OLD_DEFINITION' not in json.dumps(fresh.tools)
+            assert 'NEW_DEFINITION' in json.dumps(fresh.tools)
+    finally:db.close()
+

+def test_transition_clears_old_pin_atomically_when_later_pin_write_fails(env,monkeypatch):
+    complete(env);db=own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env):
+            old=new_agent(db);old.tools=[tool_definition('OLD_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(old,None,[])
+            same=new_agent(db);same.tools=[tool_definition('SAME_ADMISSION_DIFFERENT_BYTES')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(same,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+            assert 'OLD_DEFINITION' in json.dumps(same.tools)
+            revoke(env)
+        env.admin.set_user('default',**ident(),enabled=True,role='user')
+        with entered(env):
+            fresh=new_agent(db);fresh.tools=[tool_definition('NEW_DEFINITION')]
+            with monkeypatch.context() as m:
+                def failed(*a,**kw):raise OSError('synthetic pin commit failure')
+                m.setattr(db,'update_session_tool_names',failed)
+                conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(fresh,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+                assert db.get_session(fresh.session_id)['tool_names'] is None
+            resumed=new_agent(db);resumed.tools=[tool_definition('CURRENT_DEFINITION')]

+            assert 'OLD_DEFINITION' not in json.dumps(resumed.tools)
+            assert 'CURRENT_DEFINITION' in json.dumps(resumed.tools)
+    finally:db.close()

+"""Concrete native cache mismatch window under invalid persisted provenance."""
+import json
+from test_user_onboarding import env,complete
+from test_ordinary_skills import entered
+from test_prompt_admission import own_db,new_agent,tool_definition
+from agent import conversation_loop
+from hermes_cli import friday_prompt_scope
+
+def test_extra_provenance_key_and_failed_tool_commit(env,monkeypatch):
+    complete(env);db=own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env):
+            old=new_agent(db);old.tools=[tool_definition('OLD_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(old,None,[])
+            before=db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            assert 'OLD_DEFINITION' in before['tool_names']
+            meta=json.loads(before['model_config'])
+            meta[friday_prompt_scope.FIELD]['obsolete_extra_field']=True
+            db.update_session_meta(old.session_id,json.dumps(meta))
+            row=db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            fresh=new_agent(db);fresh.tools=[tool_definition('NEW_DEFINITION')]
+            assert not friday_prompt_scope.matches(fresh,row['system_prompt'],row)
+            with monkeypatch.context() as m:
+                def failed(*a,**kw):raise OSError('synthetic pin commit failure')
+                m.setattr(db,'update_session_tool_names',failed)
+                conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(fresh,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+                assert 'NEW_DEFINITION' in json.dumps(fresh.tools)
+            committed=db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            assert friday_prompt_scope.matches(fresh,committed['system_prompt'],committed)
+            resumed=new_agent(db);resumed.tools=[tool_definition('CURRENT_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(resumed,None,[{'role':'user','content':'continue'}])
+            observation={'invalid_extra_key_mismatch_confirmed':True,'fresh_tools_before_failed_pin':fresh.tools,
+                         'committed_tool_names':committed['tool_names'],'resumed_tools':resumed.tools,
+                         'old_pin_restored': 'OLD_DEFINITION' in json.dumps(resumed.tools)}
+            assert not observation['old_pin_restored'],'obsolete tool definition restored after invalid-provenance rebuild and failed pin commit'
+    finally:db.close()
+
+"""Native DB/restore fault windows for rejected and valid provenance.
+
+Ordinary onboarding, admission and SessionDB are real. The declared tools and
+agent prompt builder are fixture inputs; no model or deployed user is involved.
+"""
+import json
+import pytest
+from test_user_onboarding import env, complete
+from test_ordinary_skills import entered
+from test_prompt_admission import own_db, new_agent, tool_definition
+from agent import conversation_loop
+from hermes_cli import friday_prompt_scope
+
+
+def corrupt_stamp(db, agent, variant):
+    row = db.get_session(agent.session_id)
+    meta = json.loads(row['model_config'])
+    stamp = meta[friday_prompt_scope.FIELD]
+    if variant == 'missing':
+        del meta[friday_prompt_scope.FIELD]
+    elif variant == 'null':
+        meta[friday_prompt_scope.FIELD] = None
+    elif variant == 'empty_dict':
+        meta[friday_prompt_scope.FIELD] = {}
+    elif variant == 'empty_string':
+        meta[friday_prompt_scope.FIELD] = ''
+    elif variant == 'list':
+        meta[friday_prompt_scope.FIELD] = [stamp]
+    elif variant == 'missing_field':
+        del stamp['schema']
+    elif variant == 'extra_field':
+        stamp['obsolete_extra_field'] = True
+    elif variant == 'bad_digest':
+        stamp['prompt_sha256'] = '0' * 64
+    else:
+        raise AssertionError(variant)
+    db.update_session_meta(agent.session_id, json.dumps(meta))
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('variant', [
+    'missing', 'null', 'empty_dict', 'empty_string', 'list',
+    'missing_field', 'extra_field', 'bad_digest',
+])
+def test_invalid_stamp_clears_pin_in_native_prompt_transaction(env, monkeypatch, variant):
+    complete(env)
+    db = own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env):
+            old = new_agent(db)
+            old.tools = [tool_definition('OLD_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(old, None, [])
+            corrupt_stamp(db, old, variant)
+            invalid = db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            fresh = new_agent(db)
+            fresh.tools = [tool_definition('NEW_DEFINITION')]
+            assert not friday_prompt_scope.matches(fresh, invalid['system_prompt'], invalid)
+            with monkeypatch.context() as m:
+                def fail_pin(*args, **kwargs):
+                    raise OSError('synthetic separate pin write failure')
+                m.setattr(db, 'update_session_tool_names', fail_pin)
+                conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(
+                    fresh, None, [{'role': 'user', 'content': 'continue'}])
+                committed = db.get_session(fresh.session_id)
+                assert committed['tool_names'] is None
+                assert friday_prompt_scope.matches(fresh, committed['system_prompt'], committed)
+                assert 'NEW_DEFINITION' in json.dumps(fresh.tools)
+                assert json.loads(committed['model_config'])['keep_native_field'] == 'PRESERVE'
+            resumed = new_agent(db)
+            resumed.tools = [tool_definition('CURRENT_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(
+                resumed, None, [{'role': 'user', 'content': 'continue'}])
+            assert 'OLD_DEFINITION' not in json.dumps(resumed.tools)
+            assert 'CURRENT_DEFINITION' in json.dumps(resumed.tools)
+            assert db.get_messages(old.session_id)[0]['content'] == 'Continue my original task'
+    finally:
+        db.close()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('caching', [False, True])
+def test_valid_stamp_keeps_pin_across_same_admission_prompt_rewrite(env, monkeypatch, caching):
+    complete(env)
+    db = own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env):
+            old = new_agent(db, caching)
+            old.tools = [tool_definition('SAME_ADMISSION_PIN')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(old, None, [])
+            before = db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            # Native rewrites within the same valid admission retain their tool
+            # freeze even when the prompt bytes change and re-pinning then fails.
+            replacement = 'REFRESHED_SAME_ADMISSION_PROMPT'
+            db.update_system_prompt(old.session_id, replacement)
+            with monkeypatch.context() as m:
+                def fail_pin(*args, **kwargs):
+                    raise OSError('synthetic separate pin write failure')
+                m.setattr(db, 'update_session_tool_names', fail_pin)
+                with pytest.raises(OSError):
+                    db.update_session_tool_names(old.session_id, [])
+            committed = db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            assert committed['tool_names'] == before['tool_names']
+            assert friday_prompt_scope.matches(old, replacement, committed)
+            resumed = new_agent(db, caching)
+            resumed.tools = [tool_definition('CHANGED_FIXTURE_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(
+                resumed, None, [{'role': 'user', 'content': 'continue'}])
+            assert resumed._cached_system_prompt == replacement
+            resumed._build_system_prompt.assert_not_called()
+            assert 'SAME_ADMISSION_PIN' in json.dumps(resumed.tools)
+            assert 'CHANGED_FIXTURE_DEFINITION' not in json.dumps(resumed.tools)
+            assert db.get_messages(old.session_id)[0]['content'] == 'Continue my original task'
+    finally:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def test_pin_clear_rolls_back_with_failed_native_prompt_write(env, monkeypatch):
+    complete(env)
+    db = own_db(env)
+    try:
+        with entered(env):
+            old = new_agent(db)
+            old.tools = [tool_definition('OLD_DEFINITION')]
+            conversation_loop._restore_or_build_system_prompt(old, None, [])
+            corrupt_stamp(db, old, 'extra_field')
+            before = db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            with monkeypatch.context() as m:
+                def fail_prompt(*args, **kwargs):
+                    raise OSError('synthetic native prompt storage failure')
+                m.setattr(db, '_store_system_prompt', fail_prompt)
+                with pytest.raises(OSError):
+                    db.update_system_prompt(old.session_id, 'MUST_NOT_PERSIST')
+            after = db.get_session(old.session_id)
+            for field in ('system_prompt', 'model_config', 'tool_names'):
+                assert after[field] == before[field]
+            assert not friday_prompt_scope.matches(old, after['system_prompt'], after)
+    finally:
+        db.close()

+from test_user_onboarding import save
+"""Real native registered consumers; synthetic principals and secrets, no network."""
+import json
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+import pytest
+from test_user_onboarding import env, complete, home, ident

+SENTINEL='SYNTHETIC_OTHER_PRINCIPAL_SECRET'
+EXPR='${FRIDAY_REPAIR_ONLY_SENTINEL}'
+
+
+def install_document(root, body):
+    folder=root/'skills'/'procedure';folder.mkdir(mode=0o700,parents=True,exist_ok=True)
+    p=folder/'SKILL.md';p.write_text(body);p.chmod(0o600)
+    return p
+
+
+def config_document():
+    return ('---\nname: procedure\ndescription: Native config regression\nmetadata:\n  hermes:\n    config:\n'
+            '      - key: docpath\n        description: Own configured document path\n'
+            '        default: "'+EXPR+'"\n---\nOwn verified procedure.\n')
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('configured',[False,True])
+def test_real_preload_and_agent_autoload_keep_arbitrary_variables_literal(env,monkeypatch,configured):
+    env.template['config']['skills'].update({'auto_load':['procedure'],'config':{'docpath':EXPR} if configured else {}})
+    save(env,env.cfg)
+    if configured:
+        with pytest.raises(ValueError,match="ambient_templates_or_credentials_refused"):
+            complete(env)
+        return  # Real onboarding refuses ambient config before creating an admitted home.
+    complete(env);install_document(home(env),config_document())
+    monkeypatch.setenv('FRIDAY_REPAIR_ONLY_SENTINEL',SENTINEL)
+    with entered(env):
+        text,names,missing=skill_commands.build_preloaded_skills_prompt(['procedure'])
+        assert names==['procedure'] and not missing
+        assert 'docpath = '+EXPR in text and SENTINEL not in text
+        agent=SimpleNamespace(valid_tool_names=['skill_view'],session_id='native-autoload')
+        parts=system_prompt._auto_load_parts(agent)
+        assert parts and 'docpath = '+EXPR in parts[0] and SENTINEL not in ''.join(parts)
+        assert system_prompt._auto_load_parts(agent)==parts
+
+
+def test_explicit_own_paths_do_not_use_host_home_or_lookalike_variables(users,monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv('HOME','/not-the-product-user')
+    monkeypatch.setenv('HOME_SECRET',SENTINEL)
+    monkeypatch.setenv('HERMES_HOME_SECRET',SENTINEL)
+    monkeypatch.setenv('FRIDAY_REPAIR_ONLY_SENTINEL',SENTINEL)
+    with users.enter(0):
+        expected=str(users.homes[0]/'home')
+        for value in ('~','~/notes','$HOME','${HOME}','$HOME/notes','${HOME}/notes'):
+            result=skill_utils._expand_skill_config_path(value)
+            assert result==(expected+'/notes' if value.endswith('/notes') else expected)
+        assert skill_utils._expand_skill_config_path('${HERMES_HOME}/skills')==str(users.homes[0]/'skills')
+        for value in ('$HOME_SECRET','$HERMES_HOME_SECRET','${FRIDAY_REPAIR_ONLY_SENTINEL}','~another-user/docs'):
+            assert skill_utils._expand_skill_config_path(value)==value
+
+
+def revoke(env):
+    with scope.authority(env.home):
+        env.admin.set_user('default',**ident(),enabled=False,role='user')
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('bridge',['registry','model_tools'])
+def test_real_registered_view_never_returns_content_after_telemetry_revocation(env,monkeypatch,bridge):
+    complete(env);install_document(home(env),document(body='PRIVATE_CONTENT_MUST_NOT_RETURN'))
+    import model_tools
+    def interrupted(*a,**kw):
+        revoke(env)
+        scope.current()  # real sticky revocation, raised inside best-effort telemetry
+    monkeypatch.setattr(skill_usage,'bump_view',interrupted)
+    with entered(env) as cap:
+        if bridge=='registry':result=dispatch('skill_view',name='procedure')
+        else:result=json.loads(model_tools.handle_function_call('skill_view',{'name':'procedure'},task_id='revocation-native'))
+        assert not result.get('success') and result.get('error')
+        assert 'PRIVATE_CONTENT_MUST_NOT_RETURN' not in json.dumps(result)
+        assert cap.revoked
+
+
+def test_best_effort_telemetry_failure_keeps_authorized_native_view(env,monkeypatch):
+    complete(env);install_document(home(env),document(body='AUTHORIZED_PRIVATE_CONTENT'))
+    def failed(*a,**kw):raise RuntimeError('synthetic telemetry storage failure')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(skill_usage,'bump_view',failed)
+    with entered(env):
+        result=dispatch('skill_view',name='procedure')
+        assert result['success'] and 'AUTHORIZED_PRIVATE_CONTENT' in result['content']
+
+
+def test_agent_autoload_cached_return_refuses_retained_revoked_scope(env):
+    env.template['config']['skills'].update({'auto_load':['procedure']})
+    save(env,env.cfg)
+    complete(env);install_document(home(env),document(body='CACHED_PRIVATE_CONTENT'))

+        revoke(env)
+        with pytest.raises(scope.ScopeDenied):system_prompt._auto_load_parts(agent)

+def test_agent_autoload_cache_does_not_cross_two_actual_onboarded_users(env):
+    env.template['config']['skills'].update({'auto_load':['procedure']})

+        assert 'ONLY_USER_1' in first and 'ONLY_USER_2' not in first
+    with entered(env,'2'):
+        second=''.join(system_prompt._auto_load_parts(agent))
+        assert 'ONLY_USER_2' in second and 'ONLY_USER_1' not in second
